            // level in a price cluster gets a tag; the rest keep their line + engine feed
            bool crowded = false
            if array.size(liqs) > 0
                for i = 0 to array.size(liqs) - 1
                    Liq o = array.get(liqs, i)
                    if not o.dead and not na(o.lb) and math.abs(o.price - price) <= atr * 0.35
                        crowded := true
                        break
            if lqLabels and not crowded
                l.lb := label.new(bar_index + 5, price, tag + "  " + f_px(price), xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(col, 100), textcolor = color.new(col, 0), style = label.style_label_left, size = lblSzSm)
        array.push(liqs, l)
        f_trimLiq(liqs, lqMax)

bool eqHiNow = not na(phExt) and not na(msExt.prevPh) and math.abs(phExt - msExt.prevPh) <= atr * lqEqTol
bool eqLoNow = not na(plExt) and not na(msExt.prevPl) and math.abs(plExt - msExt.prevPl) <= atr * lqEqTol
if inRange
    if eqHiNow
        f_addLiq(math.max(phExt, msExt.prevPh), bar_index - msExtLen, true, "EQH", 2)
    if eqLoNow
        f_addLiq(math.min(plExt, msExt.prevPl), bar_index - msExtLen, false, "EQL", 2)
    if not na(phExt) and not eqHiNow
        f_addLiq(phExt, bar_index - msExtLen, true, "BSL", 1)
    if not na(plExt) and not eqLoNow
        f_addLiq(plExt, bar_index - msExtLen, false, "SSL", 1)
    // (HTF LIQUIDITY moved to §16c — it now rides the HTF structure machine so an HTF EQH/EQL
    //  pair can be recognised as one strong pool instead of two lone pivots. See f_htfStep.)

// Sweep scan. A sweep = wick through a level BY THE MINIMUM PENETRATION + close back inside
// (strict, always — grabs that keep closing beyond a level are BREAKS, the structure engine
// owns those). v1.1.0: penetration floor + the swept level's STRENGTH travels with the event.
bool  sweepHighEvt = false
bool  sweepLowEvt  = false
float sweepHighPx  = na
float sweepLowPx   = na
string sweepHighTag = ""
string sweepLowTag  = ""
int   sweepHighStr = 1
int   sweepLowStr  = 1
float sweepHighPen = 0.0     // deepest penetration seen this bar, per side — the sweep
float sweepLowPen  = 0.0     // reference is the level price actually RAIDED deepest
float lqPenPx      = atr * lqMinPen
var int lastSwpHiBar = na    // most recent buyside / sellside sweep — zone-grade "liquidity event" test
var int lastSwpLoBar = na

// Failed-breakout events this bar, named by IMPLICATION: fbBull = a failed break DOWN (the
// level held, sellers are offside) → bullish; fbBear = a failed break UP → bearish.
bool   fbBullEvt = false
bool   fbBearEvt = false
float  fbBullPx  = na
float  fbBearPx  = na
string fbBullTag = ""
string fbBearTag = ""
// excursion extreme of the WINNING failed level — the trap direct entry's stop anchor
float  fbBullExt = na
float  fbBearExt = na

if array.size(liqs) > 0
    for i = array.size(liqs) - 1 to 0
        Liq l = array.get(liqs, i)
        bool taken = l.buyside ? high > l.price + lqPenPx and close < l.price : low < l.price - lqPenPx and close > l.price
        // BEYOND: a bar that CLOSES past the level has filled the orders resting there. This is
        // NOT a sweep — no sweep event fires and nothing arms. Without handling it the level
        // would live in `liqs` forever: acting as an obstacle wall price has already eaten,
        // feeding the CRT liquidity weight, drawing its line, and occupying a slot in the
        // lqMax budget that then evicts a LIVE pool by insertion order.
        bool beyondNow = barstate.isconfirmed and not l.swept and (l.buyside ? close > l.price : close < l.price)
        // Detection OFF → the level is consumed at once.
        bool consumed = not fbEnable and beyondNow
        // Detection ON → PROBATION. A close beyond is only a GENUINE breakout if it HOLDS.
        // Reclaimed inside the window after a deep enough excursion = FAILED breakout: the
        // level was defended and every breakout trader is offside. Either verdict spends the
        // level. A level on probation is excluded from the obstacle gate and the CRT liquidity
        // read while it is under test — price is beyond it, so it is not a wall right now.
        bool newBreak = fbEnable and beyondNow and not l.broken
        if newBreak
            l.broken   := true
            l.breakBar := bar_index
            l.breakExt := l.buyside ? high : low
        if fbEnable and l.broken and not newBreak and barstate.isconfirmed
            l.breakExt := l.buyside ? math.max(l.breakExt, high) : math.min(l.breakExt, low)
            bool rcl   = l.buyside ? close < l.price : close > l.price
            bool penOk = math.abs(l.breakExt - l.price) >= atr * fbMinPen
            if rcl and bar_index - l.breakBar <= fbBars and penOk
                if l.buyside
                    // failed break UP → bearish. Highest failed level wins the reference.
                    if not fbBearEvt or l.price > fbBearPx
                        fbBearPx  := l.price
                        fbBearTag := l.tag
                        fbBearExt := l.breakExt
                    fbBearEvt := true
                else
                    if not fbBullEvt or l.price < fbBullPx
                        fbBullPx  := l.price
                        fbBullTag := l.tag
                        fbBullExt := l.breakExt
                    fbBullEvt := true
            // failed, late reclaim (no edge left) or a breakout that held — spent either way
            if rcl or bar_index - l.breakBar > fbBars
                consumed := true
        if taken and not l.swept
            l.swept := true
            // SEVERAL LEVELS SWEPT IN ONE BAR → the level with the DEEPEST PENETRATION wins
            // the reference (v1.2.0 P0-1). v1.1.0 kept the HIGHEST buyside / LOWEST sellside
            // PRICE — which is by construction the SHALLOWEST penetration of the group: with
            // pools at 100 and 105 and a bar high of 106 it reported the 1-point poke at 105
            // and threw away the 6-point raid through 100. The reference level drives the
            // chase gate, the range-edge test, the sweep grade and the dashboard, so the
            // engine was grading the wrong event. Ties go to the STRONGER pool — cluster
            // strength already ranks swing < EQH/EQL = session < HTF pivot < day level.
            float pen = l.buyside ? high - l.price : l.price - low
            if l.buyside
                if not sweepHighEvt or pen > sweepHighPen or (pen == sweepHighPen and l.lqStr > sweepHighStr)
                    sweepHighPx  := l.price
                    sweepHighTag := l.tag
                    sweepHighStr := l.lqStr
                    sweepHighPen := pen
                sweepHighEvt := true
                lastSwpHiBar := bar_index
            else
                if not sweepLowEvt or pen > sweepLowPen or (pen == sweepLowPen and l.lqStr > sweepLowStr)
                    sweepLowPx  := l.price
                    sweepLowTag := l.tag
                    sweepLowStr := l.lqStr
                    sweepLowPen := pen
                sweepLowEvt := true
                lastSwpLoBar := bar_index

            if lqShowPools and lqLabels and canDraw
                // solid dark chip under the sweep mark (v1.0.7) — a bare colored glyph over
                // candles / session shading was unreadable
                label.new(bar_index, l.price, "×", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(cPanel, 0), textcolor = color.new(l.buyside ? cLiqB : cLiqS, 0), style = l.buyside ? label.style_label_down : label.style_label_up, size = lblSz)
        bool dropLiq = l.swept or consumed
        if dropLiq
            f_killLiq(l)
            array.remove(liqs, i)
        if not dropLiq and heavyOk and not na(l.ln)
            line.set_x2(l.ln, bar_index + 5)
            if not na(l.lb)
                label.set_x(l.lb, bar_index + 5)

// PDH / PDL sweeps — the previous-day extremes are first-class liquidity (v1 gap: these never
// armed the engine). One sweep per level per day.
newDayLv = ta.change(pdhV) != 0 or ta.change(pdlV) != 0
// (pdFeedOk — the above-Daily engine-feed guard — is declared in section 6, above f_addLiq.)
if newDayLv
    sweptPdh := false
    sweptPdl := false
if pdFeedOk and inRange
    if not sweptPdh and not na(pdhV) and high > pdhV + lqPenPx and close < pdhV
        sweptPdh := true
        float penPdh = high - pdhV
        if not sweepHighEvt or penPdh > sweepHighPen or (penPdh == sweepHighPen and sweepHighStr < 3)
            sweepHighPx  := pdhV
            sweepHighTag := "PDH"
            sweepHighStr := 3
            sweepHighPen := penPdh
        sweepHighEvt := true
        lastSwpHiBar := bar_index
    if not sweptPdl and not na(pdlV) and low < pdlV - lqPenPx and close > pdlV
        sweptPdl := true
        float penPdl = pdlV - low
        if not sweepLowEvt or penPdl > sweepLowPen or (penPdl == sweepLowPen and sweepLowStr < 3)
            sweepLowPx  := pdlV
            sweepLowTag := "PDL"
            sweepLowStr := 3
            sweepLowPen := penPdl
        sweepLowEvt := true
        lastSwpLoBar := bar_index


// ── PDH / PDL probation — same verdict as the pool levels, day-scoped state. Day levels are
//    where overnight stops sit, so a failed break of one is the highest-information version
//    of this event. These are not members of `liqs`, hence the separate bookkeeping.
var bool  pdhBroken   = false
var int   pdhBreakBar = na
var float pdhBreakExt = na
var bool  pdlBroken   = false
var int   pdlBreakBar = na
var float pdlBreakExt = na
if newDayLv
    pdhBroken := false
    pdlBroken := false
if fbEnable and pdFeedOk and inRange and barstate.isconfirmed
    // split ifs guarded by bools — an if/else-if chain here mixes branch end types (CE10235)
    bool pdhNew = not pdhBroken and not sweptPdh and not na(pdhV) and close > pdhV
    if pdhNew
        pdhBroken   := true
        pdhBreakBar := bar_index
        pdhBreakExt := high
    if pdhBroken and not pdhNew and not na(pdhV)
        pdhBreakExt := math.max(pdhBreakExt, high)
        if close < pdhV
            if bar_index - pdhBreakBar <= fbBars and pdhBreakExt - pdhV >= atr * fbMinPen
                if not fbBearEvt or pdhV > fbBearPx
                    fbBearPx  := pdhV
                    fbBearTag := "PDH"
                    fbBearExt := pdhBreakExt
                fbBearEvt := true
            pdhBroken := false
    bool pdlNew = not pdlBroken and not sweptPdl and not na(pdlV) and close < pdlV
    if pdlNew
        pdlBroken   := true
        pdlBreakBar := bar_index
        pdlBreakExt := low
    if pdlBroken and not pdlNew and not na(pdlV)
        pdlBreakExt := math.min(pdlBreakExt, low)
        if close > pdlV
            if bar_index - pdlBreakBar <= fbBars and pdlV - pdlBreakExt >= atr * fbMinPen
                if not fbBullEvt or pdlV < fbBullPx
                    fbBullPx  := pdlV
                    fbBullTag := "PDL"
                    fbBullExt := pdlBreakExt
                fbBullEvt := true
            pdlBroken := false

// ── Rolling failed-breakout state: the confluence weight and the dashboard read this. It is
//    deliberately the ONLY thing the detection feeds — no entry, no arming, no second score.
var int    lastFbDir = 0
var int    lastFbBar = na
var string fbTxt     = "—"
if fbBullEvt
    lastFbDir := 1
    lastFbBar := bar_index
    fbTxt     := "Failed break DOWN ▲ " + fbBullTag
    if fbShowMk and canDraw
        label.new(bar_index, low, "⤫", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(cPanel, 0), textcolor = color.new(cBull, 0), style = label.style_label_up, size = lblSz)
if fbBearEvt
    lastFbDir := -1
    lastFbBar := bar_index
    fbTxt     := "Failed break UP ▼ " + fbBearTag
    if fbShowMk and canDraw
        label.new(bar_index, high, "⤫", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(cPanel, 0), textcolor = color.new(cBear, 0), style = label.style_label_down, size = lblSz)

bool fbAlignB = fbEnable and lastFbDir == 1  and not na(lastFbBar) and bar_index - lastFbBar <= fbLife
bool fbAlignS = fbEnable and lastFbDir == -1 and not na(lastFbBar) and bar_index - lastFbBar <= fbLife

// Stop-anchor overrides for the sweep feed (v1.0.8). A failed-breakout reclaim bar almost
// always doubles as a SWEEP of the same level (price closes back through it, wick beyond) —
// but the defended extreme is the MULTI-BAR excursion tracked through the probation, not the
// reclaim bar's own wick. Feeding the sweep with the bar wick alone parked the sequence stop
// INSIDE the defended excursion → premature stop-outs on every trap-armed setup. f_runSetup
// still takes min/max against the bar wick, so a deeper same-bar wick always wins anyway.
float sweepLowExtPx  = fbBullEvt and not na(fbBullExt) ? fbBullExt : na
float sweepHighExtPx = fbBearEvt and not na(fbBearExt) ? fbBearExt : na

if sweepHighEvt
    liqTxt := sweepHighTag + " swept"
if sweepLowEvt
    liqTxt := sweepLowTag + " swept"

// ══ SESSION LIQUIDITY EVENTS (v1.5.0 §16/§17) ═════════════════════════════════════════════════
//   "Asia High swept" · "London Low swept" · "NY High swept" · "London-Close High swept" — named
//   explicitly instead of being buried inside a generic pool tag, because the session narrative
//   is the context an operator actually reads: Asia builds the range, London raids it, NY raids
//   whichever of the two is still resting.
//   TWO THINGS THIS DELIBERATELY DOES NOT DO. It does not create a second liquidity event: the
//   sweep has ALREADY been counted once by §9 and this only NAMES it, so a session sweep and an
//   SMC pool sweep of the same level stay ONE event with one grade (§10 — descriptors, never
//   independent confirmations). And it never implies a direction: the engine does not assume
//   London or NY reverses. Session is context; the structure shift decides the side.
f_sessName(string t) => str.contains(t, "Asia") ? "Asia" : str.contains(t, "NYO") ? "NY" : str.contains(t, "LC") ? "London Close" : str.contains(t, "LO") ? "London" : ""
var string sessSwpTxt = "—"
var int    sessSwpBar = na
if sweepHighEvt and f_sessName(sweepHighTag) != ""
    sessSwpTxt := f_sessName(sweepHighTag) + " High swept"
    sessSwpBar := bar_index
if sweepLowEvt and f_sessName(sweepLowTag) != ""
    sessSwpTxt := f_sessName(sweepLowTag) + " Low swept"
    sessSwpBar := bar_index

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 10. ORDER BLOCKS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

f_findOrigin(Cand c, bool bullBreak, int lb) =>
    float best = bullBreak ? 1e20 : -1e20
    bool  lastMode = obPick == "Last Opposite Candle"
    bool  done = false
    for i = 1 to lb
        bool opposite = not done and (bullBreak ? close[i] < open[i] : close[i] > open[i])
        bool better   = opposite and (lastMode or (bullBreak ? low[i] < best : high[i] > best))
        if better
            best  := bullBreak ? math.min(best, low[i]) : math.max(best, high[i])
            c.idx := i
            c.top := obSource == "Body" ? math.max(open[i], close[i]) : high[i]
            c.bot := obSource == "Body" ? math.min(open[i], close[i]) : low[i]
            done  := lastMode

f_newOb(bool bull) =>
    Cand c = Cand.new()
    f_findOrigin(c, bull, obLookback)
    // POI REFINEMENT (v1.0.3): shrink the block to the boundary price actually trades into.
    // Bullish OB (origin = bearish candle, price falls INTO it from above) refines its TOP:
    // mean threshold = the 50% line, origin open = the body's top edge. Mirrored for bearish.
    // The refined edge must keep top > bot — a degenerate refinement keeps the full zone.
    if not na(c.idx) and obRefine != "Full zone"
        float oOpen = open[c.idx]
        if bull
            float refT = obRefine == "Mean threshold (50%)" ? (c.top + c.bot) / 2.0 : math.min(oOpen, c.top)
            c.top := refT > c.bot ? refT : c.top
        else
            float refB = obRefine == "Mean threshold (50%)" ? (c.top + c.bot) / 2.0 : math.max(oOpen, c.bot)
            c.bot := refB < c.top ? refB : c.bot
    if not na(c.idx) and f_engSig(c.top, c.bot) and not f_dupZone(bull ? obUp : obDn, bull, c.top, c.bot)
        Zone z = Zone.new(top = c.top, bot = c.bot, left = bar_index - c.idx, bull = bull, kind = "OB")
        // ORDER BLOCK GRADE (v1.1.0). Not every opposite candle before a break is an
        // institutional block. Three independent facts, one point each:
        //   departure  — the leg from the origin candle to this break CLOSE is itself a
        //                displacement (the block was LEFT with force, not drifted away from)
        //   break bar  — the structure-breaking bar displaced (conviction at the break)
        //   fuel       — the leg left an imbalance (an FVG born after the origin) OR a
        //                liquidity sweep preceded it within 10 bars (the block was formed by
        //                the reaction to a grab)
        // 0 = C zone (draws, never feeds stage ③ outside Aggressive) · 1 = B · 2 = A · 3 = A+
        int   obq    = 0
        float depart = math.abs(close - close[c.idx])
        obq += depart >= atr * msDispMult ? 1 : 0
        obq += displacement ? 1 : 0
        bool obLiq = bull ? f_recent(lastSwpLoBar, 10) : f_recent(lastSwpHiBar, 10)
        // SECTION-ORDER BLIND SPOT (v1.5.0). §10 runs BEFORE §11, so at order-block creation
        // time the FVG array cannot yet contain the gap whose third candle is THIS bar — and on
        // a displacement break that is exactly the gap the departure leg just printed, i.e. the
        // single most relevant imbalance there is. The "fuel" grade point was therefore
        // systematically blind to the imbalance created by the very move that formed the block.
        // The raw three-candle test is evaluated inline so the current bar's gap counts too.
        bool obGapNow = bull ? (low > high[2] and close[1] > open[1]) : (high < low[2] and close[1] < open[1])
        bool obImb = obGapNow or f_zoneSince(bull ? fvUp : fvDn, bar_index - c.idx)
        obq += (obLiq or obImb) ? 1 : 0
        z.q := obq
        f_drawZone(z, obTransp, obExtend)
        array.push(bull ? obUp : obDn, z)
        f_trimZones(bull ? obUp : obDn, obShowLast)

if inRange
    if extE.bosUp or extE.chUp
        f_newOb(true)
    if extE.bosDn or extE.chDn
        f_newOb(false)

// ── REJECTION BLOCKS — the wick zone of the confirmed EXTERNAL swing-extreme candle.
//    phExt/plExt confirm msExtLen bars after the extreme; [msExtLen] reads the pivot bar
//    itself. Upper wick at a swing high = bearish RB, lower wick at a swing low = bullish.
//    Non-repainting: the pivot is confirmed, the wick is historical fact.
// BIRTH-TIME NORMALISATION (v1.2.0 P0-6): phExt/plExt confirm msExtLen bars AFTER the
// extreme, so the wick being measured is msExtLen bars old — grading it against TODAY's
// ATR made the same candle a valid RB or noise depending on volatility that arrived after
// it printed. The gate and the grade now read the ATR of the wick's own bar.
float rbAtr = nz(atr[msExtLen], atr)
if inRange and rbEnable
    if not na(phExt)
        float rbT = high[msExtLen]
        float rbB = math.max(open[msExtLen], close[msExtLen])
        if rbT - rbB >= rbAtr * rbMinWick and not f_dupZone(rbDn, false, rbT, rbB)
            Zone z = Zone.new(top = rbT, bot = rbB, left = bar_index - msExtLen, bull = false, kind = "RB")
            // RB grade: a wick twice the minimum is a decisive rejection (2), else standard (1)
            z.q := rbT - rbB >= rbAtr * rbMinWick * 2 ? 2 : 1
            f_drawZone(z, obTransp, obExtend)
            array.push(rbDn, z)
            f_trimZones(rbDn, rbMax)
    if not na(plExt)
        float rbT2 = math.min(open[msExtLen], close[msExtLen])
        float rbB2 = low[msExtLen]
        if rbT2 - rbB2 >= rbAtr * rbMinWick and not f_dupZone(rbUp, true, rbT2, rbB2)
            Zone z = Zone.new(top = rbT2, bot = rbB2, left = bar_index - msExtLen, bull = true, kind = "RB")
            z.q := rbT2 - rbB2 >= rbAtr * rbMinWick * 2 ? 2 : 1
            f_drawZone(z, obTransp, obExtend)
            array.push(rbUp, z)
            f_trimZones(rbUp, rbMax)

// Touch-episode accounting: a touch is counted when price ENTERS the zone, not per bar
// spent inside it. A zone past its tap budget (or banned after a stop-out) keeps rendering
// but stops feeding the engine — the liquidity resting there is already consumed.
f_touchTick(Zone z, bool tagged) =>
    if tagged and not z.inTouch
        z.touches := z.touches + 1
    z.inTouch := tagged
    not z.banned and (enMaxTouch == 0 or z.touches <= enMaxTouch)

// ══ MITIGATION BLOCK (v1.5.0 §7) ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//   v1.4.0 treated MIT as a DISPLAY STATE — a grey box and a tag. An institutional mitigation
//   block is a different object: the REMAINING actionable origin after price has partially
//   consumed the zone but has NOT structurally invalidated it. Three facts have to be published
//   for that to be tradeable, and only the third existed (and only for gaps):
//     ORIGINAL bounds   what the zone was      → z.top0 / z.bot0, frozen at birth
//     REMAINING bounds  what is left to trade  → z.top / z.bot, republished here
//     FILL %            how exhausted it is    → z.fillPct, measured against the ORIGINAL span
//   `eff` is the zone's EFFECTIVE direction (a breaker trades opposite its birth side), so a
//   flipped block rebalances from the correct side.
//
//   CAUSAL ORDER: this runs AFTER the tap is published, never before. v1.4.0 shrank the gap
//   first, which republished the zone edge as THIS bar's own extreme and then filled the entry
//   there — a resting limit order can only ever have sat at the edge as it stood when the bar
//   OPENED. Publishing the tap on the pre-bar bounds and shrinking afterwards is the difference
//   between a fill that was available and a fill that was learned about after the fact.
f_mitTick(Zone z, bool eff, bool shrink) =>
    float pen = eff ? low : high
    z.extreme := na(z.extreme) ? pen : (eff ? math.min(z.extreme, pen) : math.max(z.extreme, pen))
    float t0 = nz(z.top0, z.top)
    float b0 = nz(z.bot0, z.bot)
    float sp = math.max(t0 - b0, syminfo.mintick)
    float fl = eff ? (t0 - math.max(z.extreme, b0)) / sp : (math.min(z.extreme, t0) - b0) / sp
    z.fillPct := math.min(100.0, math.max(0.0, fl * 100.0))
    if shrink and eff and z.extreme > z.bot and z.extreme < z.top
        z.top := z.extreme
    if shrink and not eff and z.extreme < z.top and z.extreme > z.bot
        z.bot := z.extreme

// Ban every live zone overlapping the stopped-out trade's entry zone — the level failed.
f_banRegion(array<Zone> a, float top, float bot) =>
    if array.size(a) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(a) - 1
            Zone z = array.get(a, i)
            if not z.dead and math.min(top, z.top) - math.max(bot, z.bot) > 0
                z.banned := true

// Lifecycle. Taps accumulate into the shared Tap objects — NEAREST tapped zone wins the
// bounds across EVERY category (OB / FVG / IFVG / breaker), not merely within one array.
// dirTap collects the zone's own direction; oppTap collects breaker retests (a broken
// bull OB retested = bearish breaker → the other side of the engine).
f_updateObs(array<Zone> a, Tap dirTap, Tap oppTap, float zAtr, int transp, int ext) =>
    if array.size(a) > 0
        for i = array.size(a) - 1 to 0
            Zone z = array.get(a, i)
            // DECISIVE CLOSE-THROUGH (v1.5.0 §6/§9). A one-tick close past an edge is not a
            // structural break of the zone. Execution-timeframe zones keep the raw test they
            // have always had; HTF zones demand 0.10 HTF-ATR, because a tenth of a CHART ATR
            // through a 4H order block is noise at the timeframe that drew the block — and a
            // breaker minted from that noise would then flip direction and vote in POI
            // selection, obstacle scanning and confluence with a full institutional grade.
            float mgn = z.htf ? zAtr * 0.10 : 0.0
            bool closedThrough = z.bull ? close < z.bot - mgn : close > z.top + mgn
            bool tagged        = f_touch(z)

            // ① INVALIDATION / BREAKER CONVERSION FIRST (v1.2.0 P0-2 — CAUSAL ORDER).
            //    v1.1.0 published the tap BEFORE this test, so a bar that wicked into a
            //    bullish OB and then CLOSED BELOW it still emitted a bullish POI tap. Because
            //    f_tapMerge keeps only the NEAREST tapped zone, that destroyed zone could win
            //    the race and hide a valid zone behind it — the trigger then failed its own
            //    rejection test and the whole setup missed the entry. A zone cannot be a valid
            //    POI on the candle that conclusively destroys it.
            // BREAKER = A NEW IDENTITY, NOT A RECOLOURED OLD ONE (§6). A bullish block broken
            // DOWNWARD is a BEARISH breaker: effective direction flips, the retest budget is
            // reset, the break bar itself may not count as its own retest, and — new in v1.5.0
            // — the mitigation history resets too. Carrying the block's old fill percentage and
            // penetration extreme into the breaker meant a zone that had been 80 % consumed
            // from above was published as 80 % consumed from BELOW, which is not a fact about
            // the breaker at all. The flip bar now lives in its own field: v1.4.0 smuggled it
            // through `extreme` (an int inside a float), which is precisely why an OB-family
            // zone could never track penetration and be a breaker at the same time.
            if (z.state == ST_FRESH or z.state == ST_MITIGATED) and closedThrough
                if obBreaker or z.htf
                    z.state   := ST_BREAKER
                    z.frozen  := na
                    z.flipBar := bar_index
                    z.touches := 0          // breaker = new identity (opposite direction) → fresh tap budget
                    z.inTouch := false
                    z.banned  := false
                    z.extreme := na
                    z.fillPct := 0.0
                    z.top0    := z.top
                    z.bot0    := z.bot
                else
                    z.state  := ST_INVALID
                    z.frozen := nz(z.frozen, bar_index)

            // reclaimed = price closed back through the ORIGINAL side → the breaker is spent,
            // and it may not publish a retest tap on the bar that spends it (same defect class)
            bool reclaimed = z.state == ST_BREAKER and (z.bull ? close > z.top + mgn : close < z.bot - mgn)

            // ② TOUCH ACCOUNTING — only a zone that SURVIVED this bar can be touched, and the
            //    touch budget is not spent by a wick into a zone the bar destroyed.
            bool alive   = (z.state == ST_FRESH or z.state == ST_MITIGATED) or (z.state == ST_BREAKER and not reclaimed and bar_index > nz(z.flipBar, bar_index))
            bool tagOk   = tagged and alive
            bool touchOk = f_touchTick(z, tagOk)

            if tagOk and touchOk and z.state != ST_BREAKER
                f_tapMerge(dirTap, z.bull, z.touches, z.top, z.bot, z.q, z.left, z.htf, z.kind)
                if z.state == ST_FRESH
                    z.state  := ST_MITIGATED
                    z.frozen := bar_index
            // a breaker trades the OPPOSITE way — its retest feeds the other side of the
            // engine and inherits the block's grade (the same institutional footprint, now
            // defended from the other side). `left` = the flip bar.
            if tagOk and touchOk and z.state == ST_BREAKER
                f_tapMerge(oppTap, not z.bull, z.touches, z.top, z.bot, z.q, int(nz(z.flipBar, z.left)), z.htf, "BRK")

            // ③ MITIGATION BLOCK — remaining bounds + fill %, republished for the NEXT bar.
            if alive
                f_mitTick(z, z.state == ST_BREAKER ? not z.bull : z.bull, true)


            // "Keep Mitigated" is a DISPLAY switch (v1.0.5) — hiding must not delete the zone
            // from the ENGINE: a deleted MIT zone could never serve its remaining tap budget
            // or flip to a breaker. Same defect class as the v1.0.1 structTxt fix.
            if z.state == ST_MITIGATED and not obShowMit
                if not na(z.bx)
                    box.delete(z.bx)
                    z.bx := na
                if not na(z.lb)
                    label.delete(z.lb)
                    z.lb := na

            bool expired = bar_index - z.left > pfZoneAge
            bool drop = expired or reclaimed or z.state == ST_INVALID
            if drop
                f_killZone(z)
                array.remove(a, i)
            if not drop and heavyOk
                f_refreshZone(z, transp, ext)

Tap tapB = Tap.new()
Tap tapS = Tap.new()
f_updateObs(obUp, tapB, tapS, atr, obTransp, obExtend)
f_updateObs(obDn, tapS, tapB, atr, obTransp, obExtend)
// rejection blocks share the full OB lifecycle: tap → mitigated, close-through → breaker
// (feeds the OTHER side), tap budget, ban rule — their retests feed stage ③ like any zone
f_updateObs(rbUp, tapB, tapS, atr, obTransp, obExtend)
f_updateObs(rbDn, tapS, tapB, atr, obTransp, obExtend)

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 11. FVG / IFVG
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

f_tryMerge(array<Zone> a, float top, float bot, int q) =>
    bool merged = false
    if fvMerge and array.size(a) > 0
        for i = array.size(a) - 1 to 0
            Zone z = array.get(a, i)
            if not z.dead and z.state == ST_FRESH and top >= z.bot and bot <= z.top
                z.top := math.max(z.top, top)
                z.bot := math.min(z.bot, bot)
                z.q   := math.max(z.q, q)      // a merged gap keeps the best grade of its parts
                merged := true
                break
    merged

f_newGap(bool bull, float top, float bot, int leftBar) =>
    // CE REFINEMENT (v1.0.3): keep only the consequent-encroachment half (CE → far edge) —
    // a tap of the stored zone then means price reached the CE, the ICT FVG entry. The
    // size/noise tests below run on the ORIGINAL gap so refinement never drops a gap that
    // was big enough before shrinking.
    float gTop = fvRefine and bull ? (top + bot) / 2.0 : top
    float gBot = fvRefine and not bull ? (top + bot) / 2.0 : bot
    array<Zone> a = bull ? fvUp : fvDn
    // FVG GRADE (v1.1.0). A three-candle gap is only evidence of institutional flow when the
    // MIDDLE candle displaced: body vs ATR, body vs its own range, and the gap's size vs ATR.
    //   0 weak (draws, no stage-③ feed outside Aggressive) · 1 standard · 2 displacement
    //   · 3 institutional (all three)
    float mBody  = math.abs(close[1] - open[1])
    float mRange = high[1] - low[1]
    int   gq     = (mBody >= atr * msDispMult ? 1 : 0) + (mRange > 0 and mBody / mRange >= 0.6 ? 1 : 0) + (math.abs(top - bot) >= atr * 0.4 ? 1 : 0)
    if f_engSig(top, bot) and math.abs(top - bot) >= atr * fvMinMult and not f_tryMerge(a, gTop, gBot, gq)
        Zone z = Zone.new(top = gTop, bot = gBot, left = leftBar, bull = bull, kind = "FVG")
        z.q := gq
        f_drawZone(z, fvTransp, fvExtend)
        array.push(a, z)
        f_trimZones(a, fvMax)

if inRange
    if low > high[2] and close[1] > open[1]
        f_newGap(true, low, high[2], bar_index - 2)
    if high < low[2] and close[1] < open[1]
        f_newGap(false, low[2], high, bar_index - 2)

f_updateGaps(array<Zone> a, Tap tb, Tap ts, float zAtr, int transp, int ext) =>
    if array.size(a) > 0
        for i = array.size(a) - 1 to 0
            Zone z = array.get(a, i)
            bool gapTagged = f_touch(z)
            bool through   = z.bull ? close < z.bot : close > z.top
            // IFVG (v1.1.0, HTF-aware in v1.5.0 §4): an inversion is a FAILED gap that the other
            // side now DEFENDS — that takes a real close-through (margin beyond the far edge)
            // WITH conviction (displacement grade ≥ 2 or body ≥ 0.5 ATR). A close one tick past
            // a stale gap used to mint a fresh "institutional inversion" zone; it is now simply
            // invalid. The margin and the conviction body are measured in the ZONE'S OWN
            // volatility: a chart bar with a 0.5-chart-ATR body is not conviction against a 4H
            // gap, and grading a 4H inversion by 5m volatility is how a one-tick graze on the
            // execution timeframe used to flip an institutional zone's direction.
            //   The inversion bar is NOT the first retest of the new identity: z.born and z.left
            //   are both reset to this bar below, and f_touch refuses a tap on the birth bar.
            bool throughDeep = z.bull ? close < z.bot - zAtr * 0.1 : close > z.top + zAtr * 0.1
            bool convict     = z.htf ? barBody >= zAtr * 0.5 : (dispQ >= 2 or barBody >= atr * 0.5)
            bool invertNow   = through and z.kind == "FVG" and fvShowIFVG and throughDeep and convict
            if invertNow
                z.kind    := "IFVG"
                z.bull    := not z.bull
                z.state   := ST_FRESH
                z.extreme := na
                z.fillPct := 0.0
                z.frozen  := na
                z.touches := 0          // inversion = new identity → fresh tap budget
                z.inTouch := false
                z.banned  := false
                z.left    := bar_index  // new identity is born on the inversion bar
                z.born    := bar_index  // …and f_touch blocks a tap on that birth bar
                z.top0    := z.top      // the IFVG is its own object — original bounds and fill
                z.bot0    := z.bot      // history restart with it, they are not the gap's
                z.q       := z.htf ? 2 : (dispQ >= 2 ? 2 : 1)
            if through and not invertNow
                z.state  := ST_INVALID
                z.frozen := nz(z.frozen, bar_index)

            // ② TOUCH AFTER INVALIDATION (v1.2.0 P0-3 — CAUSAL ORDER). A bullish gap the
            //    bar CLOSES through is not a bullish POI on that bar: it is either dead or an
            //    IFVG the other side now defends. v1.1.0 published the tap first, so the
            //    destroyed gap still fed the bullish trigger and, being nearest, hid the valid
            //    gap behind it. The inversion sets z.born = bar_index above, so the flipped
            //    zone cannot publish a tap for its NEW direction on its birth bar either —
            //    the same first-bar guard every other zone gets. Routing is by the zone's
            //    CURRENT direction: an IFVG lives in its birth array but trades the other way.
            bool gapAlive   = not through and (z.state == ST_FRESH or z.state == ST_MITIGATED)

            bool gapTagOk   = gapTagged and gapAlive
            bool gapTouchOk = f_touchTick(z, gapTagOk)
            if gapTagOk and gapTouchOk
                f_tapMerge(z.bull ? tb : ts, z.bull, z.touches, z.top, z.bot, z.q, z.left, z.htf, z.kind)
                if z.state == ST_FRESH
                    z.state  := ST_MITIGATED
                    z.frozen := bar_index

            // ③ MITIGATION BLOCK / SHRINK TO THE UNFILLED PORTION — AFTER the tap (v1.5.0 §7).
            //    v1.4.0 fixed the fact that this block had never executed at all (it required
            //    ST_FRESH while sitting below the line that sets ST_MITIGATED), but it then ran
            //    it BEFORE the tap — which republished the gap's edge as THIS bar's own extreme
            //    and filled the entry there. A resting limit order can only have sat at the edge
            //    as it stood when the bar OPENED; filling at the extreme the bar happened to
            //    reach is a price learned after the fact, and it flattered 1R on every entry
            //    that used the "Zone edge" fill. Order is now: publish the tap on the pre-bar
            //    bounds → measure this bar's penetration → republish what is LEFT for the next
            //    bar. f_mitTick also maintains fillPct against the ORIGINAL span, so a gap that
            //    has been shrunk can still reach the auto-remove threshold (in v1.4.0 the span
            //    was recomputed from the shrunken bounds, so fillPct reset toward zero on every
            //    shrink and `fvRemoveFill` became unreachable).
            if gapAlive
                f_mitTick(z, z.bull, fvShrink and fvRemoveFill >= 100)

            bool dropGap = z.state == ST_INVALID or z.fillPct >= fvRemoveFill or bar_index - z.left > pfZoneAge
            if dropGap
                f_killZone(z)
                array.remove(a, i)
            if not dropGap and heavyOk
                f_refreshZone(z, transp, ext)

f_updateGaps(fvUp, tapB, tapS, atr, fvTransp, fvExtend)
f_updateGaps(fvDn, tapB, tapS, atr, fvTransp, fvExtend)

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 12. PREMIUM / DISCOUNT + OTE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

float rHi = msExt.rangeHi
float rLo = msExt.rangeLo
bool  rOk = not na(rHi) and not na(rLo) and rHi > rLo
float eq  = rOk ? (rHi + rLo) / 2.0 : na
float rSpan = rOk ? rHi - rLo : na
// NEUTRAL STAYS NEUTRAL (v1.2.0 P0-5). `msExt.dir >= 0` folded dir == 0 — "no confirmed
// external leg yet", the state at chart start and after every break — into BULLISH, so the
// discount-side retracement band was drawn on a market with no established leg at all. A
// direction the engine has NOT established may never be silently promoted to a side.
bool  bullLeg = msExt.dir > 0
bool  legOk   = rOk and msExt.dir != 0
float fib62  = legOk ? (bullLeg ? rHi - rSpan * 0.620 : rLo + rSpan * 0.620) : na
float fib79  = legOk ? (bullLeg ? rHi - rSpan * 0.790 : rLo + rSpan * 0.790) : na
float fib705 = legOk ? (bullLeg ? rHi - rSpan * 0.705 : rLo + rSpan * 0.705) : na

var line  lnEq  = na
var line  ln705 = na
var box   bxOte = na

f_syncLine(line src, float y, int lft, color col, string st) =>
    line l = src
    if na(l)
        l := line.new(lft, y, bar_index, y, xloc = xloc.bar_index, color = col, style = st, width = 1)
    line.set_xy1(l, lft, y)
    line.set_xy2(l, bar_index + 3, y)
    line.set_color(l, col)
    l

if rOk and heavyOk
    int lft = math.max(bar_index - 150, math.min(nz(msExt.rangeHiBar, bar_index), nz(msExt.rangeLoBar, bar_index)))
    if pdShowEQ
        lnEq := f_syncLine(lnEq, eq, lft, color.new(cNeut, 25), line.style_dashed)
    if pdShowFib and legOk
        ln705 := f_syncLine(ln705, fib705, lft, color.new(cOte, 30), line.style_solid)
    if pdShowOTE and legOk
        float oTop = math.max(fib62, fib79)
        float oBot = math.min(fib62, fib79)
        if na(bxOte)
            bxOte := box.new(lft, oTop, bar_index, oBot, xloc = xloc.bar_index, border_color = color.new(cOte, 80), bgcolor = color.new(cOte, pdTransp))
        box.set_lefttop(bxOte, lft, oTop)
        box.set_rightbottom(bxOte, bar_index + 3, oBot)

inPremium  = rOk and close > eq
oteTapNow  = legOk and low <= math.max(fib62, fib79) and high >= math.min(fib62, fib79)

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 13. PREVIOUS H/L LEVELS (display)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

var array<Lvl> lvls = array.new<Lvl>()
if barstate.isfirst
    array.push(lvls, Lvl.new(name = "PDH"))
    array.push(lvls, Lvl.new(name = "PDL"))
    array.push(lvls, Lvl.new(name = "PWH"))
    array.push(lvls, Lvl.new(name = "PWL"))

f_lvlPrice(string n) =>
    switch n
        "PDH" => pdhV
        "PDL" => pdlV
        "PWH" => pwhV
        => pwlV

f_lvlShow(string n) => str.substring(n, 0, 2) == "PD" ? hlPD : hlPW

f_lvlColor(string n) => str.substring(n, 1, 2) == "D" ? cLiqB : cOte

f_syncLvl(Lvl lv, float price, bool show, bool draw) =>
    bool hide = not show or na(price)
    if hide and not na(lv.ln)
        line.delete(lv.ln)
        lv.ln := na
    if hide and not na(lv.lb)
        label.delete(lv.lb)
        lv.lb := na
    if not hide
        if price != lv.price
            lv.price  := price
            lv.broken := false
        bool isHigh = str.endswith(lv.name, "H")
        if not lv.broken and ((isHigh and close > price) or (not isHigh and close < price))
            lv.broken := true
        if draw
            color col  = f_lvlColor(lv.name)
            color colF = hlFade and lv.broken ? color.new(col, 68) : color.new(col, 25)
            color colT = hlFade and lv.broken ? color.new(col, 12) : color.new(col, 0)
            if na(lv.ln)
                lv.ln := line.new(bar_index - 1, price, bar_index, price, xloc = xloc.bar_index, extend = extend.right, color = colF, style = line.style_dashed, width = 1)
            line.set_xy1(lv.ln, bar_index - 1, price)
            line.set_xy2(lv.ln, bar_index, price)
            line.set_color(lv.ln, colF)
            if hlLabels
                if na(lv.lb)
                    // +14 bars — clear of the liquidity tags at +5, so PDH/PWH text never
                    // prints on top of a BSL/EQH tag sitting at the same price
                    lv.lb := label.new(bar_index + 14, price, lv.name, xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(col, 100), textcolor = colT, style = label.style_label_left, size = lblSzSm)
                label.set_xy(lv.lb, bar_index + 14, price)
                label.set_text(lv.lb, lv.name + "  " + f_px(price))
                label.set_textcolor(lv.lb, colT)

if array.size(lvls) > 0
    for i = 0 to array.size(lvls) - 1
        Lvl lv = array.get(lvls, i)
        f_syncLvl(lv, f_lvlPrice(lv.name), f_lvlShow(lv.name), heavyOk)

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 14. SESSIONS & KILL ZONES — closing ranges FEED the liquidity pool
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

f_inSess(simple string s) => not na(time(timeframe.period, s, tz))

inAsia = kAsiaOn and f_inSess(kAsia)
inKLon = kLonOn  and f_inSess(kLon)
inKNy  = kNyOn   and f_inSess(kNy)
inLc   = kLcOn   and f_inSess(kLc)
anyKz  = inKLon or inKNy or inLc
kzTxt  = inKNy ? "NY Open KZ" : inKLon ? "London Open KZ" : inLc ? "London Close" : inAsia ? "Asian Range" : "—"

color bgSess = anyKz ? color.new(cKzBg, 96) : inAsia ? color.new(cTok, 97) : na
bgcolor(ssBg ? bgSess : na, title = "Session BG")

// LONDON CLOSE IS A TRACKED SESSION (v1.5.0 §16). v1.4.0 shaded it, named it in the kill-zone
// row and then tracked only three ranges — so the London-close range had no high and no low,
// its extremes never became sweepable liquidity, and "London High swept" during the NY session
// could only ever mean the London OPEN range. The session most likely to be raided by the NY
// afternoon was the one the engine could not see.
var array<Sess> sessions = array.new<Sess>()
if barstate.isfirst
    array.push(sessions, Sess.new(name = "Asia", col = cTok))
    array.push(sessions, Sess.new(name = "LO",   col = cLon))
    array.push(sessions, Sess.new(name = "NYO",  col = cNy))
    array.push(sessions, Sess.new(name = "LC",   col = cLon))

// Split into independent ifs — an `if active … else if` chain here mixes an int-ending
// branch with a bool-ending branch and fails type unification (CE10235).
f_updateSess(Sess s, bool active) =>
    s.justClosed := false
    bool wasActive = s.active
    if active and not wasActive
        s.active := true
        s.hi     := high
        s.lo     := low
        s.hiBar  := bar_index
        s.loBar  := bar_index
    if active and wasActive
        if high > s.hi
            s.hi    := high
            s.hiBar := bar_index
        if low < s.lo
            s.lo    := low
            s.loBar := bar_index
    if not active and wasActive
        s.active     := false
        s.justClosed := true

if inRange and array.size(sessions) == 4
    f_updateSess(array.get(sessions, 0), inAsia)
    f_updateSess(array.get(sessions, 1), inKLon)
    f_updateSess(array.get(sessions, 2), inKNy)
    f_updateSess(array.get(sessions, 3), inLc)
    for i = 0 to 3
        Sess s = array.get(sessions, i)
        if s.justClosed and not na(s.hi)
            if ssHiLo and canDraw
                array.push(sessLns, line.new(s.hiBar, s.hi, bar_index + 30, s.hi, xloc = xloc.bar_index, color = color.new(s.col, 55), width = 1))
                array.push(sessLns, line.new(s.loBar, s.lo, bar_index + 30, s.lo, xloc = xloc.bar_index, color = color.new(s.col, 55), width = 1))
                f_trimLines(sessLns, 12)
            // v1 gap closed: the session extremes become sweepable liquidity. "NY takes the
            // Asia high then reverses" now ARMS the setup engine like any other pool.
            // sessOk (v1.1.0): a "session range" built from bars longer than an hour is one or
            // two candles wide — not a range anyone defends. Feed only on ≤ 60-minute charts.
            if lqSessFeed and sessOk
                f_addLiq(s.hi, s.hiBar, true,  s.name + "H", 2)
                f_addLiq(s.lo, s.loBar, false, s.name + "L", 2)

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 15. TIME MODEL & NEWS BLACKOUT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

nyH = hour(time, tz)
nyM = minute(time, tz)
newNyDay = ta.change(dayofmonth(time, tz)) != 0

var float midnightOpen = na
if newNyDay
    midnightOpen := open

inSilver = tmSilver and timeframe.isintraday and (nyH == 3 or nyH == 10 or nyH == 14)
bgcolor(na(bgSess) and inSilver ? color.new(DEF_ACCENT, 97) : na, title = "Silver Bullet BG")

plot(tmMidnight ? midnightOpen : na, "Midnight Open", color.new(cInk, 30), 1, plot.style_linebr)
plot(tmDayOpen  ? dOpen        : na, "Daily Open",    color.new(cLiqB, 30), 1, plot.style_linebr)

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 15b. NEWS RISK ENGINE — three SUPPORTED layers, four modes
//
//   WHAT PINE ACTUALLY HAS: request.economic() for economic data series. There is NO general
//   news API, and TradingView's Economic Calendar is a separate service that Pine cannot query.
//   So this engine never claims a live feed. It combines what is real:
//
//     LAYER A  removed in v1.4.0 — see the block below for why.
//     LAYER B  Manually scheduled events — exact timestamp, currency, impact, own pre/post
//              window. This is the layer that actually protects you from a known FOMC.
//     LAYER C  Recurring clock windows — the daily release slots, in the ⚙ reference timezone.
//
//   News is a RISK filter. It is never allowed to imply a direction: the trap / BRT / sequence
//   models do the directional work, this only decides whether the window is tradeable.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

f_impRank(string s) => s == "High" ? 3 : s == "Medium" ? 2 : 1
nwMinRank = f_impRank(nwMinImp)

// currency relevance — an event tagged USD gates EURUSD / ES / BTCUSD, not a JPY-only cross.
// Matched against the ticker id, the quote currency and the base currency.
f_curRel(string cur) => not nwCurFilt or cur == "" or str.contains(syminfo.tickerid, cur) or syminfo.currency == cur or syminfo.basecurrency == cur

minOfDay = nyH * 60 + nyM

// ── LAYER C — recurring clock windows. The HHMM list is parsed ONCE (first bar), not per bar.
var array<int>  nwClockMins = array.new<int>()
var array<bool> nwClockDone = array.new<bool>()   // per-slot "already fired today" latch
if barstate.isfirst and nwClockOn
    array<string> nwParts = str.split(str.replace_all(nwClockLs, " ", ""), ",")
    if array.size(nwParts) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(nwParts) - 1
            float hhmm = str.tonumber(array.get(nwParts, i))
            if not na(hhmm) and hhmm >= 0 and hhmm <= 2359
                array.push(nwClockMins, int(math.floor(hhmm / 100) * 60 + hhmm % 100))
                array.push(nwClockDone, false)

// reset every slot at the reference-timezone day rollover
if newNyDay and array.size(nwClockDone) > 0
    for i = 0 to array.size(nwClockDone) - 1
        array.set(nwClockDone, i, false)

bool clockRelevant = nwClockOn and timeframe.isintraday and f_curRel(nwClockCur) and f_impRank("High") >= nwMinRank
// BAR SPAN in minutes (`barMin`, section 5). The window tests below must cover the whole bar,
// not its open tick: a 1H bar opening 08:00 never has its OPEN inside an 08:30 ± 5m window,
// so the open-only test (v1.0.1) silently never blocked on charts whose bar exceeds the
// pre/post minutes.
bool nwCActive  = false
bool nwCRelease = false
if clockRelevant and array.size(nwClockMins) > 0
    for i = 0 to array.size(nwClockMins) - 1
        int m = array.get(nwClockMins, i)
        // SIGNED circular distance to the slot, in [-720, 720): positive = the release is
        // still ahead (pre-window), negative = it has passed (post-window). Plain subtraction
        // silently drops the half of a window that straddles midnight.
        int ahead = ((m - minOfDay + 2160) % 1440) - 720
        // ACTIVE when the window [slot − pre, slot + post] intersects the bar span
        // [open, open + barMin). Degenerates to the old open-only test on 1m charts.
        if ahead >= -nwPost and ahead < barMin + nwPre
            nwCActive := true
        // ONCE-PER-DAY LATCH, not a previous-bar edge test. An edge test dies across every
        // session gap: on an RTH symbol the bar before 09:30 belongs to the previous day and
        // carries a LARGER minute-of-day, so 08:30 and 09:30 would never fire at all.
        // Span-aware: fires on the bar CONTAINING the slot (or the first bar after a gap),
        // not one bar late as the open-only >= test did on higher TFs.
        if not array.get(nwClockDone, i) and minOfDay + barMin > m
            // STALENESS CAP (v1.0.8): the first bar after a LONG gap (weekend / holiday open)
            // lands hours past slots that never released while the market traded — firing
            // those as fresh releases spuriously opened the POST-NEWS-ONLY window at every
            // Sunday open and printed phantom release diamonds. A slot fires only when the
            // bar lands within the grace window; it is marked done either way (a stale slot
            // must not linger armed for the rest of the day).
            if minOfDay + barMin - m <= math.max(120, barMin + nwPost)
                nwCRelease := true
            array.set(nwClockDone, i, true)

// ── LAYER B — manually scheduled events. One helper, four call sites: [active, releaseBar].
//    Both reads are bar-SPAN aware: `act` tests window-vs-[time, time_close) intersection
//    (the open-only test never engaged on charts whose bar exceeds the pre/post minutes),
//    and `rel` is a fires-once latch on the first bar whose CLOSE time passes the event —
//    the old open-crossing edge missed events inside session gaps and fired one bar late.
f_nwEvt(bool on, int t, int pre, int post, string cur, string imp) =>
    bool ok  = on and f_impRank(imp) >= nwMinRank and f_curRel(cur)
    bool act = ok and time <= t + post * 60000 and time_close > t - pre * 60000
    var bool fired = false
    bool rel = ok and not fired and time_close > t
    if rel
        fired := true
    [act, rel]

[nwB1A, nwB1R] = f_nwEvt(nwE1On, nwE1T, nwE1Pre, nwE1Post, nwE1Cur, nwE1Imp)
[nwB2A, nwB2R] = f_nwEvt(nwE2On, nwE2T, nwE2Pre, nwE2Post, nwE2Cur, nwE2Imp)
[nwB3A, nwB3R] = f_nwEvt(nwE3On, nwE3T, nwE3Pre, nwE3Post, nwE3Cur, nwE3Imp)
[nwB4A, nwB4R] = f_nwEvt(nwE4On, nwE4T, nwE4Pre, nwE4Post, nwE4Cur, nwE4Imp)

// ── LAYER A (request.economic) WAS REMOVED IN v1.4.0. Three reasons, in order of weight:
//    (1) it can only ever see a release AS IT PRINTS — it has no forward calendar, so it can
//        never arm the PRE-news blackout that is the whole point of a news filter, and v1.4.0's
//        "the release bar is never an entry" rule now covers the one thing it did contribute;
//    (2) it is the only construct in this file that can throw a RUNTIME ERROR (a wrong
//        country/field code), which is why it shipped OFF by default and stayed off;
//    (3) request.* calls compile their entire expression tree in a secondary context and are
//        by far the most expensive thing per source line — three of the file's fourteen were
//        spent on the least useful of the three news layers, against a hard compiled-token
//        ceiling. Layers B (scheduled events) and C (recurring clock windows) are unaffected
//        and still produce every release the POST-NEWS-ONLY mode needs.

// ── COMBINE. A Layer-A release also opens a post window (it is detected AT the print, so it
//    can only ever protect the minutes AFTER the number).
var int nwLastRel    = na
bool newsRelease = nwCRelease or nwB1R or nwB2R or nwB3R or nwB4R
if newsRelease
    nwLastRel := time
bool newsWindow = nwCActive or nwB1A or nwB2A or nwB3A or nwB4A
// POST-NEWS-ONLY window: opens `nwPost` minutes AFTER the release and closes `nwPostOnly`
// minutes after it. The lower bound is the whole point of the mode — without it the release
// BAR ITSELF qualifies, which is the exact bar the mode exists to keep you out of.
int nwSinceRel  = na(nwLastRel) ? na : time - nwLastRel
bool inPostWin  = not na(nwSinceRel) and nwSinceRel >= nwPost * 60000 and nwSinceRel <= nwPostOnly * 60000

// Mode 3 needs SOME layer capable of producing a release. With none configured, nothing can
// ever open the window and the indicator would emit zero signals forever with no explanation.
bool nwHasSource = (nwClockOn and timeframe.isintraday) or nwE1On or nwE2On or nwE3On or nwE4On

// Mode 1 BLOCK · Mode 2 WARNING · Mode 3 POST-NEWS ONLY · Mode 4 IGNORE.
// `newsBlock` keeps its v3.10 name and meaning (a hard entry gate) so every downstream model
// reads it unchanged; WARNING never blocks, it only tags.
// THE RELEASE BAR IS NEVER AN ENTRY (v1.4.0 §18). WARNING mode printed entries on the very
// bar a number dropped — the first post-news spike is not a setup, it is the market repricing
// with no book, and any structure it appears to break is provisional. News never predicts
// direction here; it decides whether the volatility window is tradeable at all, and the
// release bar is the one bar where the answer is always no. BLOCK and POST-NEWS-ONLY already
// covered it; WARNING did not. IGNORE still means ignore.
newsBlock = nwMode == "Ignore" ? false : newsRelease ? true : nwMode == "Block" ? newsWindow : nwMode == "Post-news only" ? (nwHasSource and not inPostWin) : false
newsWarn  = nwMode == "Warning" and newsWindow

string nwActiveNm = nwB1A ? nwE1Nm : nwB2A ? nwE2Nm : nwB3A ? nwE3Nm : nwB4A ? nwE4Nm : nwCActive ? "clock window" : ""
newsTxt = nwMode == "Ignore" ? "filter off" : nwMode == "Post-news only" ? (not nwHasSource ? "⚠ POST-NEWS ONLY: no release source — filter disabled" : inPostWin ? "post-news window OPEN" : "waiting for release · ALL ENTRIES BLOCKED") : newsWindow ? (nwMode == "Block" ? "BLOCKED · " : "RISK · ") + nwActiveNm : "clear"

bgcolor(nwShowBg and newsWindow ? color.new(newsBlock ? cBear : DEF_ACCENT, 94) : na, title = "News Risk Window")
plotshape(nwShowMk and newsRelease, "News Release", shape.diamond, location.top, color.new(DEF_ACCENT, 20), size = size.tiny)

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 16. HTF INSTITUTIONAL CONTEXT ENGINE (v1.5.0 §3-§10) — structure · CISD · zones · liquidity
//
//   v1.4.0's "HTF engine" was a bias integer and four `if` statements that minted an ungraded,
//   lifecycle-less box. This section replaces it with the same institutional model the execution
//   timeframe already runs — and deliberately runs it THROUGH THE SAME CODE, because a second
//   parallel implementation of a zone lifecycle is a second place for the lifecycle to be wrong.
//   The HTF arrays are driven by f_updateObs / f_updateGaps / f_mitTick / f_tapMerge, so HTF
//   zones inherit, at no extra risk: FRESH → MITIGATED → BREAKER → RECLAIMED, the FVG→IFVG
//   inversion with its causal ordering, the mitigation block, the touch-episode budget, the
//   first-touch model, the ban-after-stop-out rule, POI ranking and obstacle scanning.
//
//   WHAT IS HTF-SPECIFIC, and therefore written here:
//     · every measurement is graded in HTF ATR (hAtrE), never in chart ATR;
//     · every state transition that is an HTF FACT (structure, CISD, bias, zone birth) happens
//       only on `newHtfBar` — the first chart bar after an HTF candle CLOSES;
//     · price INTERACTION with an HTF level (tap, mitigation, close-through) is a chart-bar
//       fact about price meeting a fixed higher-timeframe price, and commits on chart closes.
//
//   ALL OF IT LIVES IN ONE FUNCTION. Pine's CE10295 counts GLOBAL-scope statements and this file
//   sits near that cap; statements inside a function body are free. A function may not assign a
//   global scalar, so the engine's state lives on a `Htf` object and on `msHtf` — mutating an
//   object's fields, and pushing to a global array, are both permitted.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

type Htf
    int    cisdD  = 0        // last CONFIRMED HTF CISD direction (+1 / -1 / 0)
    int    cisdB  = na       // chart bar it confirmed on
    float  cBearO = na       // CISD reference opens, maintained on the CLOSED HTF candle series
    float  cBullO = na
    int    strD   = 0        // last HTF structure event direction
    int    strQ   = 0        // 0 none · 1 raw break · 2 BOS/CHoCH with displacement · 3 STRONG MSS
    int    strB   = na
    string strT   = "—"
    int    dispQ  = 0        // displacement grade of the last CLOSED HTF candle (0-3)
    int    nFvg   = 0        // live inventory, refreshed every bar for the dashboard
    int    nIfvg  = 0
    int    nOb    = 0
    int    nBrk   = 0
    int    nMit   = 0

var Htf     hx     = Htf.new()
var array<Zone> htObU = array.new<Zone>()   // HTF order blocks, bullish birth side
var array<Zone> htObD = array.new<Zone>()   // HTF order blocks, bearish birth side
var array<Zone> htFv  = array.new<Zone>()   // HTF gaps — mixed side; f_updateGaps routes on z.bull
htfT = math.min(96, fvTransp + 6)
// HTF candle : chart candle ratio — every HTF memory window below is expressed in it, so the
// same code self-scales from 1m→15M (15 chart bars per context candle) to 1D→1M (~21).
int htfRatio = math.max(1, int(timeframe.in_seconds(htfTf) / math.max(1, timeframe.in_seconds(timeframe.period))))

// HTF break test. Separate from f_evalBreak because it must judge the HTF CLOSE against an HTF
// margin: §9 — "require meaningful close-through rather than one-tick breaks". A raw `>` here
// would let a single tick past an HTF swing flip the bias that gates every entry in the file.
f_evalBreakH(MS m, float c, float mgn, bool disp) =>
    Evt e = Evt.new()
    if not na(m.ph) and c > m.ph + mgn
        if m.dir == 1
            e.bosUp := true
        else if m.dir == -1
            e.chUp  := true
            e.mssUp := disp
        e.pxUp := m.ph
        m.dir  := 1
        m.ph   := na
    else if not na(m.pl) and c < m.pl - mgn
        if m.dir == -1
            e.bosDn := true
        else if m.dir == 1
            e.chDn  := true
            e.mssDn := disp
        e.pxDn := m.pl
        m.dir  := -1
        m.pl   := na
    e

// PER-SIDE, PER-FAMILY TRIM (v1.5.0). v1.4.0 trimmed one shared HTF array with a single FIFO,
// so three bullish zones in a row evicted every bearish one and the "Max HTF zones per side"
// input never meant what it said — the engine could be structurally blind to one whole side.
f_trimSide(array<Zone> a, bool bull, int cap) =>
    int n = 0
    int idx = -1
    if array.size(a) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(a) - 1
            Zone zz = array.get(a, i)
            if zz.bull == bull
                n += 1
                if idx < 0
                    idx := i
    if n > cap and idx >= 0
        f_killZone(array.get(a, idx))
        array.remove(a, idx)

// ══ THE HTF BAR STEP — everything that may only change when an HTF candle CLOSES ══════════════
f_htfStep(Htf x, MS m) =>
    if newHtfBar and inRange
        float hb = math.abs(hC1 - hO1)
        float hr = hH1 - hL1
        x.dispQ := (hb >= hAtrE * msDispMult ? 1 : 0) + (hr > 0 and hb / hr >= 0.6 ? 1 : 0) + (hr >= hAtrE * 1.2 ? 1 : 0)
        bool hDisp2 = x.dispQ >= 2

        // ── ① HTF STRUCTURE: BOS / CHoCH / MSS (§9) ───────────────────────────────────────────
        //    f_pushPivot is reused verbatim, which is what gives the HTF machine the prevPh /
        //    prevPl relation memory it needs for EQH/EQL below — v1.4.0 wrote those two fields
        //    and never read them anywhere.
        f_pushPivot(m, hPh, hPl, 0)
        Evt he = f_evalBreakH(m, hC1, hAtrE * 0.1, hDisp2)
        //    STRONG MSS (grade 3) is the grade that unlocks A+ on the HTF context, so it demands
        //    what a repricing actually looks like: a CHoCH (not a continuation BOS), grade-3
        //    displacement on the breaking candle, AND a close that cleared the protected swing
        //    by a quarter HTF-ATR. Everything else with displacement is 2; a drift across a
        //    swing is 1. Same three-tier vocabulary as the LTF shift grade, one timeframe up.
        if he.bosUp or he.chUp
            x.strD := 1
            x.strQ := (he.mssUp and x.dispQ >= 3 and (hC1 - nz(he.pxUp, hC1)) >= hAtrE * 0.25) ? 3 : (hDisp2 ? 2 : 1)
            x.strB := bar_index
            x.strT := (he.chUp ? (x.strQ >= 3 ? "MSS ▲" : "CHoCH ▲") : "BOS ▲") + " · d" + str.tostring(x.dispQ)
        if he.bosDn or he.chDn
            x.strD := -1
            x.strQ := (he.mssDn and x.dispQ >= 3 and (nz(he.pxDn, hC1) - hC1) >= hAtrE * 0.25) ? 3 : (hDisp2 ? 2 : 1)
            x.strB := bar_index
            x.strT := (he.chDn ? (x.strQ >= 3 ? "MSS ▼" : "CHoCH ▼") : "BOS ▼") + " · d" + str.tostring(x.dispQ)

        // ── ② HTF CISD (§8) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        //    Computed on the CLOSED HTF candle sequence and nowhere else, so on a 1m chart this
        //    is a 15M CISD, on 5m a 1H CISD, on 15m a 5H CISD — the dashboard prints it by that
        //    name. Reference = the open of the FIRST candle of the opposing close-series (the
        //    ICT definition), and it is SPENT when it fires so price oscillating around one
        //    stale open cannot refire forever. CONVICTION IS MANDATORY: a tiny close across an
        //    old reference open is not institutional delivery, it is a tick. `hC1[1]` is the
        //    previous CLOSED HTF candle's close — the crossing edge — because hC1 only changes
        //    value on newHtfBar.
        bool hBullC = hC1 > hO1
        bool hBearC = hC1 < hO1
        bool hConv  = hb >= hAtrE * 0.5 or hDisp2
        bool cU = not na(x.cBearO) and hC1 > x.cBearO and nz(hC1[1], hC1) <= x.cBearO and hBullC and hConv
        bool cD = not na(x.cBullO) and hC1 < x.cBullO and nz(hC1[1], hC1) >= x.cBullO and hBearC and hConv
        if cU
            x.cisdD  := 1
            x.cisdB  := bar_index
            x.cBearO := na
        if cD
            x.cisdD  := -1
            x.cisdB  := bar_index
            x.cBullO := na
        if hBearC and (na(x.cBearO) or nz(hC1[1], hC1) > nz(hO1[1], hO1))
            x.cBearO := hO1
        if hBullC and (na(x.cBullO) or nz(hC1[1], hC1) < nz(hO1[1], hO1))
            x.cBullO := hO1

        // ── ③ HTF FVG (§4) ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        //    Three CLOSED HTF candles, a minimum meaningful size in HTF ATR, and a grade earned
        //    at birth from the middle candle's conviction — the same three tests the LTF grader
        //    applies, measured one timeframe up. A gap that fails the size floor is not drawn and
        //    never enters POI selection: an HTF "gap" thinner than a fifth of an HTF ATR is a
        //    rounding artefact of the higher timeframe, not an inefficiency anyone rebalances.
        float gpB = hL1 - hH3
        float gpS = hL3 - hH1
        float mB  = math.abs(hC2 - hO2)
        float mR  = hH2 - hL2
        int   mq  = (mB >= hAtrE * msDispMult ? 1 : 0) + (mR > 0 and mB / mR >= 0.6 ? 1 : 0)
        if gpB >= hAtrE * fvMinMult and hC2 > hO2 and not f_dupZone(htFv, true, hL1, hH3)
            Zone z = Zone.new(top = hL1, bot = hH3, left = math.max(0, bar_index - htfRatio * 3), bull = true, kind = "FVG", q = math.min(3, mq + (gpB >= hAtrE * 0.4 ? 1 : 0)), htf = true)
            f_drawZone(z, htfT, fvExtend)
            array.push(htFv, z)
            f_trimSide(htFv, true, htMax)
        if gpS >= hAtrE * fvMinMult and hC2 < hO2 and not f_dupZone(htFv, false, hL3, hH1)
            Zone z = Zone.new(top = hL3, bot = hH1, left = math.max(0, bar_index - htfRatio * 3), bull = false, kind = "FVG", q = math.min(3, mq + (gpS >= hAtrE * 0.4 ? 1 : 0)), htf = true)
            f_drawZone(z, htfT, fvExtend)
            array.push(htFv, z)
            f_trimSide(htFv, false, htMax)

        // ── ④ HTF ORDER BLOCK (§5) ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        //    NOT "every opposite candle". The origin candle must have been LEFT with force and
        //    the departure must have consequences:
        //      departure   the very next HTF candle closes beyond the origin's opposite extreme
        //                  AND that leg is itself a displacement (body ≥ the ⑨ ATR multiple, or
        //                  displacement grade ≥ 2)
        //      liquidity   the origin candle raided the prior HTF candle's extreme and closed
        //                  back inside it — the block was formed by the reaction to a grab
        //      structure   an HTF BOS/CHoCH/MSS landed on this same HTF candle
        //      imbalance   the departure printed an HTF FVG
        //    GRADE at birth, in HTF volatility: 0 weak · 1 standard · 2 strong · 3 institutional.
        bool dep  = math.abs(hC1 - hC2) >= hAtrE * msDispMult or hDisp2
        bool strNow = nz(x.strB, -1) == bar_index
        bool liqB = hL2 < hL3 and hC2 > hL3
        bool liqS = hH2 > hH3 and hC2 < hH3
        if hC2 < hO2 and hC1 > hH2 and dep
            float oT = obSource == "Body" ? math.max(hO2, hC2) : hH2
            float oB = obSource == "Body" ? math.min(hO2, hC2) : hL2
            float rT = obRefine == "Mean threshold (50%)" ? (oT + oB) / 2.0 : obRefine == "Origin open" ? math.min(hO2, oT) : oT
            oT := rT > oB ? rT : oT
            if oT - oB >= hAtrE * noiseFactor and not f_dupZone(htObU, true, oT, oB)
                Zone z = Zone.new(top = oT, bot = oB, left = math.max(0, bar_index - htfRatio * 2), bull = true, kind = "OB", q = math.min(3, (hDisp2 ? 1 : 0) + ((liqB or (strNow and x.strD == 1)) ? 1 : 0) + (gpB > 0 ? 1 : 0)), htf = true)
                f_drawZone(z, htfT, obExtend)
                array.push(htObU, z)
                f_trimSide(htObU, true, htMax)
        if hC2 > hO2 and hC1 < hL2 and dep
            float oT = obSource == "Body" ? math.max(hO2, hC2) : hH2
            float oB = obSource == "Body" ? math.min(hO2, hC2) : hL2
            float rB = obRefine == "Mean threshold (50%)" ? (oT + oB) / 2.0 : obRefine == "Origin open" ? math.max(hO2, oB) : oB
            oB := rB < oT ? rB : oB
            if oT - oB >= hAtrE * noiseFactor and not f_dupZone(htObD, false, oT, oB)
                Zone z = Zone.new(top = oT, bot = oB, left = math.max(0, bar_index - htfRatio * 2), bull = false, kind = "OB", q = math.min(3, (hDisp2 ? 1 : 0) + ((liqS or (strNow and x.strD == -1)) ? 1 : 0) + (gpS > 0 ? 1 : 0)), htf = true)
                f_drawZone(z, htfT, obExtend)
                array.push(htObD, z)
                f_trimSide(htObD, false, htMax)

        // ── ⑤ HTF LIQUIDITY (§10) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        //    Confirmed HTF pivots are where higher-timeframe stops rest — the pool the execution
        //    engine most wants to see swept. Ranked by structural importance: a lone HTF swing
        //    is strength 3, an HTF EQUAL HIGH/LOW pair is strength 4 (two rejections at one
        //    price is a bigger book than one), and f_addLiq adds another 3 when the level also
        //    sits on the day extreme. Registered only while still on the FAR side of price — a
        //    pivot confirmed late and already traded through is not resting liquidity, and
        //    registering it would print a phantom failed breakout the moment it is scanned.
        if not na(hPh) and hPh > close
            bool eqh = not na(m.prevPh) and math.abs(hPh - m.prevPh) <= hAtrE * lqEqTol
            f_addLiq(eqh ? math.max(hPh, m.prevPh) : hPh, bar_index - 1, true, htfLbl + (eqh ? "-EQH" : "-H"), eqh ? 4 : 3)
        if not na(hPl) and hPl < close
            bool eql = not na(m.prevPl) and math.abs(hPl - m.prevPl) <= hAtrE * lqEqTol
            f_addLiq(eql ? math.min(hPl, m.prevPl) : hPl, bar_index - 1, false, htfLbl + (eql ? "-EQL" : "-L"), eql ? 4 : 3)

    // ── LIVE INVENTORY (every chart bar) — what the context timeframe currently has on offer.
    x.nFvg  := 0
    x.nIfvg := 0
    x.nOb   := 0
    x.nBrk  := 0
    x.nMit  := 0
    for k = 0 to 2
        array<Zone> aa = k == 0 ? htFv : k == 1 ? htObU : htObD
        if array.size(aa) > 0
            for i = 0 to array.size(aa) - 1
                Zone z = array.get(aa, i)
                if not z.dead
                    if z.state == ST_BREAKER
                        x.nBrk := x.nBrk + 1
                    if z.state != ST_BREAKER
                        if z.state == ST_MITIGATED
                            x.nMit := x.nMit + 1
                        if z.kind == "IFVG"
                            x.nIfvg := x.nIfvg + 1
                        if z.kind == "FVG"
                            x.nFvg := x.nFvg + 1
                        if z.kind == "OB"
                            x.nOb := x.nOb + 1

f_htfStep(hx, msHtf)
// HTF zone lifecycle — the SAME functions the execution zones run, graded in HTF volatility.
// Price meeting a fixed higher-timeframe level is a chart-bar fact, so mitigation, inversion and
// breaker conversion commit on chart closes; only zone BIRTH is gated on the HTF candle close.
f_updateObs(htObU, tapB, tapS, hAtrE, htfT, obExtend)
f_updateObs(htObD, tapS, tapB, hAtrE, htfT, obExtend)
f_updateGaps(htFv, tapB, tapS, hAtrE, htfT, fvExtend)

// hysteresis: close must clear the EMA by a neutral band — hovering at the EMA is NEUTRAL,
// not a bias that flips every HTF bar (Balanced tolerates neutral, so no signal starvation)
float htfEmaBand = na(hAtr) ? 0.0 : hAtrE * htEmaBuf
htfEmaBias = not htfTfOk or na(hEma) ? 0 : hC1 > hEma + htfEmaBand ? 1 : hC1 < hEma - htfEmaBand ? -1 : 0
htfBias    = msHtf.dir != 0 ? msHtf.dir : htfEmaBias
htfBiasTxt = not htfTfOk ? "⚠ " + htfLbl + " ≤ chart TF — HTF engine OFF" : htfBias == 1 ? "Bullish" : htfBias == -1 ? "Bearish" : "Neutral"
// HTF CISD authority window — three CONTEXT candles, expressed in chart bars so one constant
// self-scales across every rung of the ladder. Beyond it the delivery it described is history.
bool  htCisdLive = htfTfOk and hx.cisdD != 0 and not na(hx.cisdB) and bar_index - hx.cisdB <= htfRatio * 3
int   htCisdDir  = htCisdLive ? hx.cisdD : 0
// HTF structure authority window — same clock. `strQ` is the grade, `strD` the direction.
bool  htStrLive  = htfTfOk and hx.strD != 0 and not na(hx.strB) and bar_index - hx.strB <= htfRatio * 4
int   htStrDir   = htStrLive ? hx.strD : 0
string htStrTxt  = not htfTfOk or hx.strD == 0 ? "—" : hx.strT + (htStrLive ? "" : " · stale")
string htCisdTxt = not htfTfOk ? "—" : hx.cisdD == 0 ? "—" : htfLbl + " CISD " + (hx.cisdD == 1 ? "▲" : "▼") + " · " + str.tostring(bar_index - hx.cisdB) + "b" + (htCisdLive ? "" : " · stale")

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 16b. CRT RANGE ENGINE — Candle Range Theory, full range model
//
//   The model is the PREVIOUS CLOSED candle of the CRT reference timeframe (input ㉒ — its own
//   timeframe, independent of the ⑨ bias HTF). That candle publishes a range: high, low, mid,
//   size, upper half, lower half. The CURRENT reference candle is then tracked bar by bar
//   through six lifecycle states:
//
//     SWEEP        wick clears a range extreme by ≥ the minimum penetration (manipulation)
//     RECLAIM      a close comes back inside the range     (manipulation confirmed complete)
//     EXPANSION    a close lands BEYOND the extreme        (continuation, not manipulation)
//     FAILURE      a reclaimed level is lost again         (the read was wrong — drop it)
//     STATE        bullish  = low swept + reclaimed · bearish = high swept + reclaimed
//     SCORE        0-100 over the weights in play — how much authority the state carries
//
//   Everything commits on CONFIRMED chart bars, from an expr[1] + lookahead_on reference read.
//   Nothing here reads TBS: CRT is upstream of TBS and never the other way round.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

crtTf = crtTfMode == "Engine HTF" ? htfTf : crtTfMode == "Custom" ? crtTfCust : crtTfMode
[crH1, crL1, crC1, crO1, crTm] = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, crtTf, [high[1], low[1], close[1], open[1], time[1]], lookahead = barmerge.lookahead_on)
newCrtBar = ta.change(crTm) != 0 and not na(crTm)

// FAIL CLOSED when the reference timeframe is BELOW the chart timeframe. `lookahead_on` on a
// LOWER timeframe returns a value from INSIDE the developing chart bar — future data. The
// dashboard MTF rows already guard this exact case (mtfOk1…5); the engine must too, because a
// silently-leaking CRT range would backtest beautifully and fall apart live.
crtTfOk = timeframe.in_seconds(crtTf) >= timeframe.in_seconds(timeframe.period)
// One flag for every downstream pool and gate: CRT counts as a LIVE FEED only when enabled
// AND its reference TF is valid. With the engine hard-off its weights must LEAVE the pools
// (an unearnable weight silently depresses every score against the tier floor) and the TBS
// 'CRT must agree' gate must read "feed absent", not "suppress TBS forever".
crtUsable = crtEnable and crtTfOk

// ── RANGE MODEL of the previous closed reference candle (published for every other module) ────
crtRangeHi  = crtTfOk ? crH1 : na
crtRangeLo  = crtTfOk ? crL1 : na
crtRangeMid = na(crtRangeHi) or na(crtRangeLo) ? na : (crtRangeHi + crtRangeLo) / 2.0
crtUpperHalf = not na(crtRangeMid) and close > crtRangeMid
crtLowerHalf = not na(crtRangeMid) and close < crtRangeMid

// the swept extreme sitting ON a tracked liquidity pool = more resting orders behind the sweep
f_crtLiqAt(float px) =>
    bool hit = false
    if array.size(liqs) > 0 and not na(px)
        for i = 0 to array.size(liqs) - 1
            Liq l = array.get(liqs, i)
            // a level on breakout probation is not resting liquidity right now — price is
            // beyond it and the verdict is pending
            if not l.dead and not l.broken and math.abs(l.price - px) <= atr * 0.15
                hit := true
                break
    hit

// a same-direction gap inside the manipulation leg (range extreme → sweep extreme)
f_crtFvgAt(bool wantBull, float top, float bot) =>
    bool hit = false
    if not na(top) and not na(bot) and top > bot
        array<Zone> a = wantBull ? fvUp : fvDn
        if array.size(a) > 0
            for i = 0 to array.size(a) - 1
                Zone z = array.get(a, i)
                if not z.dead and z.bull == wantBull and math.min(top, z.top) - math.max(bot, z.bot) > 0
                    hit := true
                    break
    hit

var int   crtState    = 0     // +1 bullish · -1 bearish · 0 none, this reference candle
var int   crtScore    = 0
var bool  crtSwpHi    = false
var bool  crtSwpLo    = false
var bool  crtRcHi     = false // high swept AND reclaimed → bearish
var bool  crtRcLo     = false // low swept AND reclaimed  → bullish
var bool  crtExpUp    = false
var bool  crtExpDn    = false
var bool  crtFail     = false
var float crtSwpHiExt = na
var float crtSwpLoExt = na
var int   crtHiBar    = na    // most recent bar that SWEPT the range high
var int   crtLoBar    = na
var int   crtRcHiBar  = na    // bar the high-sweep was RECLAIMED — what the state actually is
var int   crtRcLoBar  = na
var int   crtStateBar = na    // bar the current state formed — the veto's age clock
var bool  crtStrUp    = false // a same-direction structure shift landed inside this candle
var bool  crtStrDn    = false
var bool  crtDispLo   = false // the BULLISH reclaim arrived with displacement
var bool  crtDispHi   = false // the BEARISH reclaim arrived with displacement
var bool  crtLiqLo    = false // the swept range LOW sat on a tracked liquidity pool
var bool  crtLiqHi    = false
var int   crtBarOpen  = na

if newCrtBar
    crtState    := 0
    crtScore    := 0
    crtSwpHi    := false
    crtSwpLo    := false
    crtRcHi     := false
    crtRcLo     := false
    crtExpUp    := false
    crtExpDn    := false
    crtFail     := false
    crtSwpHiExt := na
    crtSwpLoExt := na
    crtHiBar    := na
    crtLoBar    := na
    crtRcHiBar  := na
    crtRcLoBar  := na
    crtStateBar := na
    crtStrUp    := false
    crtStrDn    := false
    crtDispLo   := false
    crtDispHi   := false
    crtLiqLo    := false
    crtLiqHi    := false
    crtBarOpen  := bar_index

if extE.mssUp or extE.chUp or intE.chUp
    crtStrUp := true
if extE.mssDn or extE.chDn or intE.chDn
    crtStrDn := true

float crtPen = atr * crtMinPen
float crtRcl = atr * crtMinRcl
if barstate.isconfirmed and crtEnable and not na(crtRangeHi) and not na(crtRangeLo)
    // ① SWEEP — the wick must CLEAR the extreme AND the bar must CLOSE BACK INSIDE the range
    //   (v1.3.0 P0-5). A one-tick graze is noise; a CLOSE beyond the extreme is ACCEPTANCE, and
    //   acceptance is expansion, not manipulation — the exact distinction §9 already draws
    //   between a sweep and a breakout, which the CRT engine was the last module not to make.
    //   v1.2.0 set crtSwpHi on a bar that closed ABOVE the range high, so the next close back
    //   inside was scored as a completed "manipulation → distribution" and published a BEARISH
    //   CRT state — which then vetoed every BUY for the rest of the reference candle off what
    //   was actually a bullish breakout and its pullback. (A multi-bar poke-and-reclaim is not
    //   lost information: §9 classifies it as a FAILED BREAKOUT, which is its correct name.)
    //    The liquidity read is captured HERE, on the sweep bar, because section 9 deletes a
    //    level from `liqs` the moment it is swept — scanning later always misses it.
    if low < crtRangeLo - crtPen and close > crtRangeLo
        crtSwpLo    := true
        crtLoBar    := bar_index
        crtSwpLoExt := na(crtSwpLoExt) ? low : math.min(crtSwpLoExt, low)
        crtLiqLo    := crtLiqLo or f_crtLiqAt(crtRangeLo) or (sweepLowEvt and not na(sweepLowPx) and math.abs(sweepLowPx - crtRangeLo) <= atr * 0.15)
    if high > crtRangeHi + crtPen and close < crtRangeHi
        crtSwpHi    := true
