bool crtCausalB = crtLiveBull and suBuy.state  != SU_IDLE and ((not na(crtLoBar) and nz(suBuy.sweepBar,  -1) >= crtLoBar and nz(suBuy.sweepExt,   1e20) <= crtRangeLo + atr * 0.25) or crtHtfB)
bool crtCausalS = crtLiveBear and suSell.state != SU_IDLE and ((not na(crtHiBar) and nz(suSell.sweepBar, -1) >= crtHiBar and nz(suSell.sweepExt, -1e20) >= crtRangeHi - atr * 0.25) or crtHtfS)
// ── TBS ↔ HTF (§15). Whether the TBS manipulation happened inside higher-timeframe inventory is
//    a DESCRIPTOR of that one liquidity event, shown on the ㉓ dashboard row. It deliberately
//    earns NO weight: the TBS score already prices the setup and the HTF Context category
//    already prices the HTF zone, so paying a third time for the overlap of the two would be
//    the exact double-count §10 and §19 exist to prevent.
bool tbsHtfB = tbB.st == 2 and f_htfConfl(true,  nz(tbB.lvl, close), nz(tbB.ext, close)) > 0
bool tbsHtfS = tbS.st == 2 and f_htfConfl(false, nz(tbS.ext, close), nz(tbS.lvl, close)) > 0

// ══ CONFLUENCE SCORE (v1.1.0) — EIGHT CAPPED CATEGORIES ═══════════════════════════════════════
//   This is a CONFLUENCE score, not a probability. It RANKS setups the structural gates have
//   already validated; it never manufactures validity (the grade below is structural first).
//   Correlated evidence lives in ONE category with a CAP, so four copies of "direction
//   context" (HTF bias · HTF zone · CRT · OTE) can no longer stack to most of the pool.
//   Pool fairness kept: a category part that cannot be earned (HTF feed off, no volume feed,
//   session invalid, gate already mandatory) leaves the denominator.
//   v1.5.0 §19 — THE HTF CONTEXT CATEGORY IS REBUILT, AND ITS CAP DOES NOT MOVE. HTF bias,
//   HTF structure, HTF CISD, the nested HTF zone and the CRT are all statements about ONE thing
//   (what the higher timeframe is doing) so they stay inside ONE capped category. Adding four
//   new HTF facts as four new independent confirmations would be exactly the double-count this
//   architecture exists to prevent — the extra facts REDISTRIBUTE the 20 points, they do not
//   add any. Note also that HTF CISD earns nothing when it merely repeats the HTF structure
//   event that produced it (§8: "must NOT double-count the same structural event as HTF
//   MSS/BOS") — the caller passes it as SEPARATE only when it landed on a different bar.
//     1 HTF context      bias 6 · HTF structure 5 · HTF CISD (separate) 3 · nested HTF zone
//                        by grade 2/4/6 · CRT causal 8 / directional 4              → cap 20
//     2 Liquidity        sweep grade 0/5/10/15 · failed-breakout align +3           → cap 15
//     3 Structure        shift grade 5/10/15                                        → cap 15
//     4 Displacement     shift-bar displacement grade 0/3/7/10                      → cap 10
//     5 POI              zone grade 0/6/11/15 · leg-OTE +4 · EQ side +2             → cap 15
//     6 Trigger          close location 0/3/5 · body/range 0/3/5 · TBS align +2     → cap 10
//     7 Session/Volume   kill zone 6 · volume 4                                     → cap 10
//     8 Risk/Reward      capped RR ≥ target 5 · ≥ 1.5 3 · ≥ 1.0 1                   → cap 5
f_quality(Setup s, bool dirBull, bool eqSide, int htfZq, bool cisdSep, bool crtLive, bool crtCausal, bool tbsAl, bool fbAl, float rrCap) =>
    // 1 HTF context — a weight whose condition is already a mandatory gate leaves the pool
    int hP = 0
    int hC = 0
    if htfTfOk
        // bias leaves the pool when ⑩ 'HTF bias must align' makes it mandatory — the gate
        // already guarantees it, and paying for a guaranteed fact is a floor, not a measurement
        if not effNeedHtf
            hC += 6
            hP += (dirBull ? htfBias == 1 : htfBias == -1) ? 6 : 0
        // HTF STRUCTURE — a live BOS/CHoCH/MSS in the trade's direction, weighted by its grade
        hC += 5
        hP += (dirBull ? htStrDir == 1 : htStrDir == -1) ? (hx.strQ >= 3 ? 5 : hx.strQ == 2 ? 3 : 1) : 0
        // HTF CISD — paid ONLY when it is a SEPARATE event from the HTF structure break (§8)
        hC += 3
        hP += (cisdSep and (dirBull ? htCisdDir == 1 : htCisdDir == -1)) ? 3 : 0
        // NESTED HTF ZONE — the execution POI sits inside a live HTF zone, graded by that
        // zone's own institutional quality instead of v1.4.0's flat "an HTF zone exists" 5
        hC += 6
        hP += htfZq >= 3 ? 6 : htfZq == 2 ? 4 : htfZq >= 1 ? 2 : 0
    if crtUsable
        hC += 8
        hP += crtCausal ? 8 : crtLive ? 4 : 0
    // 2 liquidity — `fbAl` is only paid when the failed breakout is a DIFFERENT liquidity
    //   event from the one that armed this setup; the caller strips it otherwise (§3).
    int lP = (s.sweepQ >= 3 ? 15 : s.sweepQ == 2 ? 10 : s.sweepQ == 1 ? 5 : 0) + (fbAl ? 3 : 0)
    int lC = 15 + (fbEnable ? 3 : 0)
    // 3 structure
    int sP = s.shiftQ >= 3 ? 15 : s.shiftQ == 2 ? 10 : s.shiftQ == 1 ? 5 : 0
    int sC = 15
    // 4 displacement of the shift bar
    int dP = s.shiftDispQ >= 3 ? 10 : s.shiftDispQ == 2 ? 7 : s.shiftDispQ == 1 ? 3 : 0
    int dC = 10
    // 5 POI
    int pP = (s.poiQ >= 3 ? 15 : s.poiQ == 2 ? 11 : s.poiQ == 1 ? 6 : 0) + (s.oteQ >= 3 ? 4 : s.oteQ == 2 ? 3 : s.oteQ == 1 ? 1 : 0) + ((not effNeedPd and eqSide) ? 2 : 0)
    int pC = 15 + 4 + ((effNeedPd or not rOk) ? 0 : 2)
    // 6 trigger bar
    float tc = dirBull ? nz(s.trigClv, 0.5) : 1.0 - nz(s.trigClv, 0.5)
    float tb = nz(s.trigBodyR, 0.0)
    int tP = (tc >= 0.7 ? 5 : tc >= 0.55 ? 3 : 0) + (tb >= 0.5 ? 5 : tb >= 0.35 ? 3 : 0) + (tbsAl ? 2 : 0)
    int tC = 10 + (tbsEnable ? 2 : 0)
    // 7 session / participation — unearnable parts leave the pool
    int eP = 0
    int eC = 0
    if sessOk and not effKzOnly
        eC += 6
        eP += (anyKz or inSilver) ? 6 : 0
    if hasVol
        eC += 4
        eP += volOkNow ? 4 : 0
    // 8 risk / reward to the first wall (no wall in the path = the full target is reachable)
    float rrE = na(rrCap) ? trRR : rrCap
    int rP = rrE >= trRR ? 5 : rrE >= 1.5 ? 3 : rrE >= 1.0 ? 1 : 0
    int rC = 5
    int num = math.min(hP, QC_HTF) + math.min(lP, QC_LIQ) + math.min(sP, QC_STR) + math.min(dP, QC_DISP) + math.min(pP, QC_POI) + math.min(tP, QC_TRIG) + math.min(eP, QC_SESS) + math.min(rP, QC_RR)
    int den = math.min(hC, QC_HTF) + math.min(lC, QC_LIQ) + math.min(sC, QC_STR) + dC + math.min(pC, QC_POI) + math.min(tC, QC_TRIG) + math.min(eC, QC_SESS) + rC
    den > 0 ? math.round(100.0 * num / den) : 100

// EQ side judged on the tapped ZONE midpoint — the same verdict the PD gate uses
float zMidB  = (tapTopB + tapBotB) / 2.0
float zMidS  = (tapTopS + tapBotS) / 2.0
bool eqSideB = rOk and not na(zMidB) and zMidB <= eq
bool eqSideS = rOk and not na(zMidS) and zMidS >= eq

// SAME-EVENT DESCRIPTORS DO NOT SCORE TWICE (v1.4.0 §3). A failed breakout landing on the
// setup's OWN arming bar is not a second piece of evidence — it is the same liquidity action
// described by a second engine, and v1.0.8 deliberately routes exactly that bar into the
// sweep feed, so it is the common case. The sweep GRADE already prices that event inside the
// capped Liquidity category; paying `fbAl` on top double-counted it. Identical reasoning for
// a TBS reclaim confirmed on the arming bar (it can even BE the arm, via ㉓ "TBS arms the
// engine"). A failed breakout or TBS on a DIFFERENT bar is genuinely separate evidence and
// still earns its weight.
bool fbSepB  = fbAlignB and nz(lastFbBar, -1) != nz(suBuy.sweepBar,  -2)
bool fbSepS  = fbAlignS and nz(lastFbBar, -1) != nz(suSell.sweepBar, -2)
bool tbsSepB = tbsAlignB and nz(tbsBar, -1) != nz(suBuy.sweepBar,  -2)
bool tbsSepS = tbsAlignS and nz(tbsBar, -1) != nz(suSell.sweepBar, -2)
// ONE HTF STRUCTURAL EVENT, PAID ONCE (v1.5.0 §8). An HTF CISD very often IS the HTF MSS —
// the same closed context candle both delivers through the reference open and breaks the swing.
// Paying the HTF Context category for both would be the identical defect the LIQUIDITY EVENT
// IDENTITY model exists to stop, one timeframe up: two engines describing one action are not
// two confirmations. The CISD weight is only earned when it landed on a DIFFERENT bar from the
// structure event. (The CISD VETO below is unaffected — a veto is not a score.)
bool htCisdSep = htCisdLive and nz(hx.cisdB, -1) != nz(hx.strB, -2)
qBuy  = f_quality(suBuy,  true,  eqSideB, htfZqB, htCisdSep, crtLiveBull, crtCausalB, tbsSepB, fbSepB, rrCapB)
qSell = f_quality(suSell, false, eqSideS, htfZqS, htCisdSep, crtLiveBear, crtCausalS, tbsSepS, fbSepS, rrCapS)
// ══ HTF CONTEXT GRADE 0-3 (§24 dashboard · §20 A+ gate) ═══════════════════════════════════════
//   How much of the institutional picture actually agrees with this direction. It is a READOUT
//   and a GATE, not a score input — every component below is already priced inside the capped
//   HTF Context category, and adding it to the score as well would double-count all four.
f_htCtxQ(bool dirBull) =>
    int q = 0
    q += (dirBull ? htfBias == 1 : htfBias == -1) ? 1 : 0
    q += (dirBull ? htStrDir == 1 : htStrDir == -1) ? 1 : 0
    q += (dirBull ? htCisdDir == 1 : htCisdDir == -1) ? 1 : 0
    q += (dirBull ? not na(htPoiTB) : not na(htPoiBS)) ? 1 : 0
    htfTfOk ? math.min(3, q) : 0
int htCtxB = f_htCtxQ(true)
int htCtxS = f_htCtxQ(false)

// ══ SETUP GRADE vs EXECUTION GRADE — the final grade is the WORSE of the two (§11, v1.4.0) ══
//   SETUP GRADE answers "why should this trade exist?" — liquidity event, causal structure
//   shift, displacement, POI, context. It is measured against the volatility of the EVENT.
//     A+  score ≥ A+ · sweep STRONG+ · shift STRONG MSS · POI ≥ 2 · FIRST touch · clean path · HTF aligned
//     A   score ≥ A  · sweep NORMAL+ · shift NORMAL+ · POI ≥ 1
//     B   score ≥ B  · shift NORMAL+   (Aggressive allows WEAK — that preset's contract is the widest feed)
//     C   everything else (prints only with the floor at "C (no filter)")
//   EXECUTION GRADE answers "is this still a good ENTRY?" — see f_execQ: filled at the POI,
//   risk inside the ceiling, path to the target unobstructed. Measured in CURRENT volatility.
//   A+ demands a perfect execution (3/3), A demands 2/3, B demands 1/3 — and an execution of
//   0 is a REJECT no matter how good the setup was. This is the §26 rule made structural: a
//   missing critical component downgrades the trade, it is never offset by weak confirmations.
//   v1.5.0 §20: A+ additionally requires an HTF CONTEXT GRADE of at least 2 — the higher
//   timeframe must agree on at least two of {bias, structure, CISD, an actionable POI on the
//   correct side}. This is a STRUCTURAL requirement, not another point in the score: "a weak
//   component must not be compensated by many weak components", and the single most common way
//   a high-scoring setup fails is that everything about it is right except the context it is
//   being taken against.
f_gradeSeq(Setup s, int score, bool dirBull, int off, int lpq, int exq, int htCtx) =>
    bool htfAl = htfTfOk and (dirBull ? htfBias == 1 : htfBias == -1)
    int minShiftB = isAgr ? 1 : 2
    int setupR = score >= qTierAp - off and s.sweepQ >= 2 and s.shiftQ >= 3 and s.poiQ >= 2 and s.poiTch <= 1 and lpq >= 2 and htfAl and htCtx >= 2 ? 3 : score >= qTierA - off and s.sweepQ >= 1 and s.shiftQ >= 2 and s.poiQ >= 1 ? 2 : score >= qTierB - off and s.shiftQ >= minShiftB ? 1 : 0
    int g = math.min(setupR, exq)      // FINAL = min(setup, execution) — §11 / §26
    g >= 3 ? "A+" : g == 2 ? "A" : g == 1 ? "B" : "C"
f_gradeRank(string g) => g == "A+" ? 3 : g == "A" ? 2 : g == "B" ? 1 : 0
// PRECISION mode (v1.4.0 §23) raises the score thresholds instead of lowering them, demands a
// STRONG MSS, a grade-2 POI and a flawless execution. "Aggressive" is retained under its own
// name — renaming an input option value silently resets every chart that already saved it —
// but it is a RESEARCH preset by contract (it switches off the HTF, PD and kill-zone gates and
// fires on a bare zone tap); it is not an institutional live mode.
int    modeOff  = isAgr ? 20 : isBal ? 10 : isPrec ? -10 : 0
int    minRank  = qMinTier == "A+" ? 3 : qMinTier == "A" ? 2 : qMinTier == "B" ? 1 : 0
minRank := isPrec ? math.max(minRank, 2) : minRank
int    execQB   = f_execQ(true,  tapTopB, gRiskB, lpqB)
int    execQS   = f_execQ(false, tapBotS, gRiskS, lpqS)
string gradeB   = f_gradeSeq(suBuy,  qBuy,  true,  modeOff, lpqB, execQB, htCtxB)
string gradeS   = f_gradeSeq(suSell, qSell, false, modeOff, lpqS, execQS, htCtxS)
bool   gradeOkB = f_gradeRank(gradeB) >= minRank
bool   gradeOkS = f_gradeRank(gradeS) >= minRank

// Final gates: HTF alignment (optional), kill-zone-only (optional), chop guard (sideways
// regime), news blackout, direction-aware cooldown, close-confirmation, no same-bar flip.
// Balanced accepts a NEUTRAL HTF (bias only vetoes when it actively OPPOSES the trade);
// Strict demands active alignment; Aggressive drops the gate.
htfOkB  = not effNeedHtf or (isBal ? htfBias >= 0 : htfBias == 1)
htfOkS  = not effNeedHtf or (isBal ? htfBias <= 0 : htfBias == -1)
// HTF CISD CONFLICT (v1.5.0 §8) — a confirmed change in the state of delivery on the CONTEXT
// timeframe, pointing the other way and still inside its authority window, is the higher
// timeframe having just repriced against this trade. Structure and bias can both lag it: a
// bullish HTF bias set by a break four candles ago survives an HTF bearish CISD that has only
// just printed, which is precisely the moment an execution BUY is most wrong. Gated behind
// ⑨ 'HTF CISD conflict blocks entry' AND ⑩ 'HTF bias must align', so a user who deliberately
// switched the HTF gate off is not re-gated through the back door. It is a VETO and earns no
// score — the HTF Context category already prices CISD agreement.
htCisdOkB = not (htCisdGate and effNeedHtf) or htCisdDir != -1
htCisdOkS = not (htCisdGate and effNeedHtf) or htCisdDir != 1
// TBS PREMISE STILL STANDING (v1.5.0 §15). With ã 'TBS arms the setup engine' on, a setup can
// be armed BY a TBS reclaim. If that TBS is later INVALIDATED — price closed back beyond the
// swept extreme, i.e. the reclaim failed — the liquidity event the sequence rests on has been
// disproved, and the state machine that knows it is the TBS record, not the sequence. Without
// this the sequence kept its arm and could still trigger on a premise its own source engine had
// already retracted. Only bites setups whose sweepTag IS 'TBS'.
tbsOkB = suBuy.sweepTag  != "TBS" or not tbB.inv
tbsOkS = suSell.sweepTag != "TBS" or not tbS.inv
// Kill zones are intraday constructs — on D/W/M charts the session test can never pass, so
// the gate would mute the indicator forever with no explanation (same silent-mute class the
// POST-NEWS-ONLY no-source fallback guards). Fail OPEN off intraday.
kzOk    = not effKzOnly or not sessOk or anyKz or inSilver
coolB   = na(lastBuyBar)  or bar_index - lastBuyBar  >= effCooldown
coolS   = na(lastSellBar) or bar_index - lastSellBar >= effCooldown

// CRT veto: an ACTIVE opposing CRT on the HTF candle blocks the entry — buying while the
// HTF candle just swept its previous high and rejected is buying into distribution.
// A state below the ㉒ minimum score never vetoes — it is display-only context.
crtOkB = not crtVeto or not crtLiveBear
crtOkS = not crtVeto or not crtLiveBull

// Signal-bar quality (all trigger modes): no BUY on a strong bearish displacement bar
// (falling knife), and the trigger bar must close out of its own bottom 40% — a rejection
// candle that never rejected is not a trigger. Mirrored for SELLs.
float sigClv = clv
barOkB = not (bearBar and displacement) and sigClv >= 0.40
barOkS = not (bullBar and displacement) and sigClv <= 0.60

// ── PER-MODEL REGIME GATES (v1.1.0). One generic "block while ranging" filter treated a
//    range-edge reversal, a trend continuation and an expansion fade identically.
//    SEQUENCE  never in DEAD; in RANGE only when the sweep took the range EDGE (the only
//              liquidity a range defends) unless the chop block is off
//    BRT       TREND / EXPANSION, or TRANSITION with a displacement arm — never RANGE / DEAD
//    TRAP      never DEAD; in EXPANSION only an INSTITUTIONAL trap (fading a leg in progress
//              on a weak reclaim is catching a knife)
bool seqRegOkB = regime != RG_DEAD and (regime != RG_RANGE or not effRfBlock or suBuy.edge)
bool seqRegOkS = regime != RG_DEAD and (regime != RG_RANGE or not effRfBlock or suSell.edge)
bool brRegOkL  = regime == RG_TREND or regime == RG_EXP or (regime == RG_TRANS and brFDispL)
bool brRegOkS  = regime == RG_TREND or regime == RG_EXP or (regime == RG_TRANS and brFDispS)

// ── DAILY CIRCUIT BREAKER — intraday risk cap, reference-timezone days. Entries count on
//    print (including reversals that replaced a live trade); losses count FULL stop-outs
//    only (break-even exits are scratches, not losses). Inert on non-intraday charts, where
//    a "day" is a single bar. NOTE: the loss counter increments in the manage block below,
//    so a stop-out cannot veto a fresh signal on its own exit bar — one-bar tolerance.
var int dayTrades = 0
var int daySlLoss = 0
if newNyDay
    dayTrades := 0
    daySlLoss := 0
bool dayOk = (rkMaxTrades == 0 or dayTrades < rkMaxTrades) and (rkMaxLoss == 0 or daySlLoss < rkMaxLoss)

// ── DUPLICATE SUPPRESSION (v1.2.0). One market event may print ONE signal. A sequence is
//    spent by its own print, but the SAME liquidity grab can arm a trap entry AND a sequence
//    that enters three bars later on the retrace — two positions, one event. Every printed
//    entry records the liquidity-event bar behind it; a sequence still resting on that event
//    is refused. Trap / BRT / CRT entries consume their own trigger by construction.
var int lastEvtB = na
var int lastEvtS = na
bool dupOkB = na(lastEvtB) or nz(suBuy.sweepBar,  -1) != lastEvtB
bool dupOkS = na(lastEvtS) or nz(suSell.sweepBar, -1) != lastEvtS

buySig  = trigBuyRaw  and htfOkB and htCisdOkB and tbsOkB and kzOk and seqRegOkB and crtOkB and barOkB and stopOkB and obstOkB and not newsBlock and coolB and dayOk and lpOkB and riskOkB and dupOkB and gradeOkB and barstate.isconfirmed
sellSig = trigSellRaw and htfOkS and htCisdOkS and tbsOkS and kzOk and seqRegOkS and crtOkS and barOkS and stopOkS and obstOkS and not newsBlock and coolS and dayOk and lpOkS and riskOkS and dupOkS and gradeOkS and barstate.isconfirmed
// DIRECTION TIE (v1.3.0 P0-9). Two opposite triggers on one bar were resolved by CODE ORDER —
// the SELL always won, for no reason beyond being written second, and the BUY was destroyed
// even when it was the A+ side against a bare-minimum B. Every other tie in this file resolves
// on evidence (deepest penetration · stronger pool · later reclaim · deeper excursion); this
// one now resolves on the entry GRADE, with the confluence score breaking a grade tie.
bool tieBuy = f_gradeRank(gradeB) * 1000 + qBuy >= f_gradeRank(gradeS) * 1000 + qSell
buySig  := buySig and (not sellSig or tieBuy)
sellSig := sellSig and not buySig

// ── OBSTACLE TEST for the direct-entry models — same wall logic as obstOkB/S above, but the
//    caller supplies the risk (each model measures risk off its OWN stop anchor).
// GATE RR == BOOKED RR (v1.3.0 P0-7). v1.1.0 fixed this for the SEQUENCE — measure the gate off
// the projected FILL, not off the close — and left the other three models measuring off the
// close while filling somewhere else entirely: BRT fills AT the broken level, the CRT retest
// fills AT its level. Risk and room-to-the-wall were therefore numbers no trade ever booked
// (for a BRT long the fill sits BELOW the close, so the gate over-stated risk and refused good
// continuations, while the room to the wall was measured from the wrong end). The fill now
// travels into the gate, so what the gate approves is what the trade records.
f_obstOk(bool dirB, float fillPx, float riskV) =>
    bool ok = true
    if enObstMode != "Off" and enObstMult != 0 and not na(riskV) and not na(fillPx)
        float wall = dirB ? obstAbove : obstBelow
        if not na(wall)
            if enObstMode == "Veto entry"
                ok := (dirB ? wall - fillPx : fillPx - wall) >= riskV * enObstMult
            else
                float capT = dirB ? wall - atr * OBST_TGT_BUF : wall + atr * OBST_TGT_BUF
                float rr   = dirB ? (math.min(fillPx + riskV * trRR, capT) - fillPx) / riskV : (fillPx - math.max(fillPx - riskV * trRR, capT)) / riskV
                ok := rr >= enObstMult
    ok

// ── ⑳ BRT ENTRY (v1.0.4): the retest trigger fired and cleared its own score floor — apply
//    the shared risk gates, risk measured off the retest-extreme stop. Trend-continuation
//    model: second in priority, right behind the sequence.
float brStopL = na(brExtL) ? na : brExtL - atr * trStopBuf
float brStopS = na(brExtS) ? na : brExtS + atr * trStopBuf
// the limit-style fill the BRT entry actually books — same formula as the entry block below
float brFillL = na(brLvlL) ? na : (low  <= brLvlL ? math.min(open, brLvlL) : close)
float brFillS = na(brLvlS) ? na : (high >= brLvlS ? math.max(open, brLvlS) : close)
float brRiskL = na(brStopL) or na(brFillL) ? na : math.max(math.abs(brFillL - brStopL), atr * 0.25)
float brRiskS = na(brStopS) or na(brFillS) ? na : math.max(math.abs(brFillS - brStopS), atr * 0.25)
bool brBuy  = brTrigL and not na(brRiskL) and f_riskOk(brRiskL) and htfOkB and kzOk and brRegOkL and crtOkB and barOkB and not newsBlock and coolB and dayOk and f_obstOk(true, brFillL, brRiskL) and not buySig and not sellSig
bool brSell = brTrigS and not na(brRiskS) and f_riskOk(brRiskS) and htfOkS and kzOk and brRegOkS and crtOkS and barOkS and not newsBlock and coolS and dayOk and f_obstOk(false, brFillS, brRiskS) and not buySig and not sellSig and not brBuy
// BRT grade for label / tier stats: 5 = A+ · 4 = A · 3 = B · below = C
f_gradeBr(int q) => q >= 5 ? "A+" : q >= 4 ? "A" : q >= 3 ? "B" : "C"

// ── TRAP QUALITY SCORE (v1.0.6 — the v3.9 engine, restored on the failed-breakout detection):
//    confluence measured at the EXCURSION (trapped level → trap extreme), 0-100 over the
//    weights in play. Computed once, on the event bar the entry uses — trivially frozen.
f_trapZone(array<Zone> a, bool wantBull, int wantState, float top, float bot) =>
    bool hit = false
    if array.size(a) > 0 and not na(top) and not na(bot)
        for i = 0 to array.size(a) - 1
            Zone z = array.get(a, i)
            if not z.dead and z.bull == wantBull and z.state == wantState and math.min(top, z.top) - math.max(bot, z.bot) > 0
                hit := true
                break
    hit

// pool-fair denominator: HTF weight leaves without HTF zones (or an invalid bias TF), breaker
// weight when breakers are off, volume without a feed, kill-zone off-intraday
// POOL FAIRNESS FOLLOWS THE ENGINE, NOT THE DRAWINGS (v1.5.0). The HTF weight used to leave the
// denominator when `htShowZones` was off — correct then, because the toggle also deleted the
// zones. Now that it governs only the boxes, the weight is fully EARNABLE with the drawings off,
// and keeping the old test would have quietly INFLATED every trap score for anyone who hid the
// HTF boxes. Pool membership now keys on the feed being valid, which is the actual condition.
int fqDen = fqOb + (htfTfOk ? fqHtf : 0) + fqFvg + (obBreaker ? fqBrk : 0) + fqMit + (hasVol ? fqVol : 0) + (sessOk ? fqKz : 0) + fqEq
// ONE SCORER, BOTH SIDES (v1.5.0). v1.4.0 carried two hand-mirrored copies of this block. They
// happened to agree, but hand-mirrored arithmetic is where asymmetry bugs live — and the pair
// cost ~400 compiled tokens in a file that is at its ceiling. Generalised on `dirB`, the two
// sides are now symmetric by CONSTRUCTION and cannot drift apart. Breaker direction is still
// handled correctly: a bullish trap fade is fuelled by broken BEAR blocks (mirrored).
f_trapScore(bool dirB, float exT, float exB, string tag) =>
    array<Zone> obA = dirB ? obUp : obDn
    array<Zone> rbA = dirB ? rbUp : rbDn
    array<Zone> obO = dirB ? obDn : obUp
    array<Zone> rbO = dirB ? rbDn : rbUp
    int fs = 0
    fs += (f_trapZone(obA, dirB, ST_FRESH, exT, exB) or f_trapZone(rbA, dirB, ST_FRESH, exT, exB)) ? fqOb : 0
    fs += f_htfConfl(dirB, exT, exB) > 0 ? fqHtf : 0
    fs += (f_trapZone(fvUp, dirB, ST_FRESH, exT, exB) or f_trapZone(fvDn, dirB, ST_FRESH, exT, exB) or f_trapZone(fvUp, dirB, ST_MITIGATED, exT, exB) or f_trapZone(fvDn, dirB, ST_MITIGATED, exT, exB)) ? fqFvg : 0
    fs += (f_trapZone(obO, not dirB, ST_BREAKER, exT, exB) or f_trapZone(rbO, not dirB, ST_BREAKER, exT, exB)) ? fqBrk : 0
    fs += (f_trapZone(obA, dirB, ST_MITIGATED, exT, exB) or f_trapZone(rbA, dirB, ST_MITIGATED, exT, exB)) ? fqMit : 0
    fs += volOkNow ? fqVol : 0
    fs += (sessOk and (anyKz or inSilver)) ? fqKz : 0
    fs += str.contains(tag, "EQ") ? fqEq : 0
    fqDen > 0 ? int(math.round(100.0 * fs / fqDen)) : 100

int trapScB = 0
int trapScS = 0
if fbBullEvt and not na(fbBullExt)
    trapScB := f_trapScore(true, fbBullPx, fbBullExt, fbBullTag)
if fbBearEvt and not na(fbBearExt)
    trapScS := f_trapScore(false, fbBearExt, fbBearPx, fbBearTag)

// ── ⑲ TRAP DIRECT ENTRY (v1.0.3, re-added as a SNIPER model): enter ON the reclaim bar of a
//    failed breakout — stop beyond the excursion extreme, the tightest stop in the toolkit.
//    Shares every risk gate with the sequence; the HTF-bias gate is included on purpose (the
//    v3.8.1 lesson: counter-trend trap fades need that gate most of all). Events are already
//    confirmed-bar-only by construction in section 9. v1.0.6: own score floor (fbMinScr).
float trapStopB = na(fbBullExt) ? na : fbBullExt - atr * trStopBuf
float trapStopS = na(fbBearExt) ? na : fbBearExt + atr * trStopBuf
float trapRiskB = na(trapStopB) ? na : math.max(math.abs(close - trapStopB), atr * 0.25)
float trapRiskS = na(trapStopS) ? na : math.max(math.abs(close - trapStopS), atr * 0.25)
// TRAP GRADE (v1.1.0). A failed breakout alone is an OBSERVATION, not a reversal.
//   0 WEAK          reclaim bar without conviction → no entry (the observation still feeds
//                   the sequence as a sweep and the Liquidity category as alignment)
//   1 VALID         reclaim bar with conviction: body/range ≥ 0.5 · body ≥ 0.3 ATR · close in
//                   the reclaiming 40 % of the bar · closes in the trade direction
//   2 INSTITUTIONAL valid + displacement grade ≥ 2 + a same-direction structure event
//                   (internal/external CHoCH, BOS or conviction CISD) on or within 3 bars
// Strict takes INSTITUTIONAL only; Balanced / Aggressive take VALID. The zone-confluence
// score (fbMinScr) still applies on top — grade says "is it a trap", score says "how good".
var int lastStrUpBar = na
var int lastStrDnBar = na
if intE.chUp or extE.chUp or extE.bosUp or cisdBullShift
    lastStrUpBar := bar_index
if intE.chDn or extE.chDn or extE.bosDn or cisdBearShift
    lastStrDnBar := bar_index
bool trapConvB = bullBar and bodyRatio >= 0.5 and barBody >= atr * 0.3 and clv >= 0.6
bool trapConvS = bearBar and bodyRatio >= 0.5 and barBody >= atr * 0.3 and clv <= 0.4
int  trapQB = not fbBullEvt or not trapConvB ? 0 : (dispQ >= 2 and f_recent(lastStrUpBar, 3)) ? 2 : 1
int  trapQS = not fbBearEvt or not trapConvS ? 0 : (dispQ >= 2 and f_recent(lastStrDnBar, 3)) ? 2 : 1
int  trapMinQ = isBal or isAgr ? 1 : 2
bool trapRegOkB = regime != RG_DEAD and (regime != RG_EXP or trapQB >= 2)
bool trapRegOkS = regime != RG_DEAD and (regime != RG_EXP or trapQS >= 2)
f_gradeTrap(int q, int sc) => q >= 2 and sc >= qTierA ? "A" : q >= 1 ? "B" : "C"
bool trapBuy  = fbEntry and fbBullEvt and trapQB >= trapMinQ and trapScB >= fbMinScr and not na(trapRiskB) and f_riskOk(trapRiskB) and htfOkB and kzOk and trapRegOkB and crtOkB and barOkB and not newsBlock and coolB and dayOk and f_obstOk(true, close, trapRiskB) and barstate.isconfirmed and not buySig and not sellSig and not brBuy and not brSell
bool trapSell = fbEntry and fbBearEvt and trapQS >= trapMinQ and trapScS >= fbMinScr and not na(trapRiskS) and f_riskOk(trapRiskS) and htfOkS and kzOk and trapRegOkS and crtOkS and barOkS and not newsBlock and coolS and dayOk and f_obstOk(false, close, trapRiskS) and barstate.isconfirmed and not buySig and not sellSig and not brBuy and not brSell and not trapBuy

// (㉒ THE CRT RETEST ENTRY MODEL WAS REMOVED IN v1.5.0. Budget was the trigger, but it is the
//  model this file should give up first on its own merits, and the ㉒ CRT RANGE ENGINE itself —
//  the range model, the lifecycle states, the score, the veto, the box, the alerts, the causal
//  alignment credit — is untouched. Four reasons, in order of weight:
//    (1) it shipped OFF and stayed off, so no live behaviour changes;
//    (2) it fires a SECOND entry off the very swept extreme the CRT state was built on. §10 is
//        explicit that one physical liquidity event may produce one entry, and this model had
//        no link to the duplicate-suppression ledger at all — `lastEvtB` only guards the NEXT
//        bar, and the sequence's own retrace off that identical raid could still print;
//    (3) it bypassed the ENTRY QUALITY GRADE entirely. Every other model resolves through
//        f_gradeSeq / f_gradeBr / f_gradeTrap and answers to the ⑪ grade floor; this one wrote
//        f_grade(crtScore) — a raw score tier — so it could and did print below the floor the
//        user set;
//    (4) it required NO structure event of any kind: no MSS, no CISD, no shift, no graded POI.
//        A touch of a price with a live CRT state was the whole entry. §18's mandatory sequence
//        and §27's 'do not let a score rescue a missing structural event' both refuse that.)

// One entry per bar. Priority: SEQUENCE > BRT > TRAP.
bool entryBrt  = brBuy or brSell
bool entryTrap = trapBuy or trapSell
bool entryBuy  = buySig or brBuy or trapBuy
bool entrySell = sellSig or brSell or trapSell

// ── LAST-VETO telemetry: a raw trigger fired but a gate blocked the print. Record WHICH
//    gate (first failure in gate order) so tuning starts from data, not guesswork.
var string lastVetoTxt = "—"
var int    lastVetoBar = na
if barstate.isconfirmed and trigBuyRaw and not buySig
    lastVetoTxt := "BUY " + (not htfOkB ? (htfTfOk ? "HTF_CONFLICT" : "HTF_ZONE_INVALID") : not htCisdOkB ? "HTF_CISD_CONFLICT" : not tbsOkB ? "TBS_WEAK" : not kzOk ? "SESSION" : not seqRegOkB ? "REGIME_" + regimeTxt : not crtOkB ? "CRT_CONFLICT" : not barOkB ? "BAR_QUALITY" : not stopOkB ? "STOP_BREACHED" : not lpOkB ? "BAD_LIQUIDITY_PATH" : not riskOkB ? "RISK_TOO_WIDE" : not obstOkB ? (wallHtfB ? "HTF_POI_OPPOSING" : "LOW_RR") : newsBlock ? "NEWS_RISK" : not coolB ? "COOLDOWN" : not dayOk ? "DAILY_CAP" : not dupOkB ? "DUPLICATE" : not gradeOkB ? "GRADE_" + gradeB + "_Q" + str.tostring(qBuy) + "_E" + str.tostring(execQB) + "_H" + str.tostring(htCtxB) : "TIE")
    lastVetoBar := bar_index
if barstate.isconfirmed and trigSellRaw and not sellSig
    lastVetoTxt := "SELL " + (not htfOkS ? (htfTfOk ? "HTF_CONFLICT" : "HTF_ZONE_INVALID") : not htCisdOkS ? "HTF_CISD_CONFLICT" : not tbsOkS ? "TBS_WEAK" : not kzOk ? "SESSION" : not seqRegOkS ? "REGIME_" + regimeTxt : not crtOkS ? "CRT_CONFLICT" : not barOkS ? "BAR_QUALITY" : not stopOkS ? "STOP_BREACHED" : not lpOkS ? "BAD_LIQUIDITY_PATH" : not riskOkS ? "RISK_TOO_WIDE" : not obstOkS ? (wallHtfS ? "HTF_POI_OPPOSING" : "LOW_RR") : newsBlock ? "NEWS_RISK" : not coolS ? "COOLDOWN" : not dayOk ? "DAILY_CAP" : not dupOkS ? "DUPLICATE" : not gradeOkS ? "GRADE_" + gradeS + "_Q" + str.tostring(qSell) + "_E" + str.tostring(execQS) + "_H" + str.tostring(htCtxS) : "TIE")
    lastVetoBar := bar_index

// SETUP TIER — thresholds are the ⑪ inputs, so the label, the alert and the print floor all
// speak one vocabulary. C is by definition below the floor: if a C prints, the floor is off.
f_grade(float s) => s >= qTierAp ? "A+" : s >= qTierA ? "A" : s >= qTierB ? "B" : "C"

// ── open / manage the tracked trade ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
f_closeTradeLines(Trade t) =>
    if not na(t.lnE)
        line.delete(t.lnE)
        t.lnE := na
    if not na(t.lnS)
        line.delete(t.lnS)
        t.lnS := na
    if not na(t.lnT)
        line.delete(t.lnT)
        t.lnT := na

// record one closed trade into the performance counters (±0.05R around zero = break-even)
// MAE / MFE (v1.4.0 §22). Realized R alone cannot tell you WHY a trade lost. MAE (maximum
// adverse excursion, in R) and MFE (maximum favourable excursion) separate the four failure
// modes that all look identical in the P/L column: a bad SETUP never goes anywhere (MFE ≈ 0,
// MAE ≈ −1); a bad ENTRY is right but too early (MAE −0.9 then MFE +3); a stop that is too
// TIGHT shows MAE just past −1 on winners-that-weren't; a target too FAR shows MFE +1.8 on
// trades that closed at break-even. Both are recorded against the FROZEN initial risk, so a
// break-even stop move cannot distort them.
f_recTrade(Stats st, float r, int dir, float mae, float mfe) =>
    st.n      := st.n + 1
    st.sumMae := st.sumMae + mae
    st.sumMfe := st.sumMfe + mfe
    st.sumR := st.sumR + r
    if r > 0.05
        st.w := st.w + 1
    if dir == 1
        st.nB    := st.nB + 1
        st.sumRB := st.sumRB + r
        st.wB    := st.wB + (r > 0.05 ? 1 : 0)
    else
        st.nS    := st.nS + 1
        st.sumRS := st.sumRS + r
        st.wS    := st.wS + (r > 0.05 ? 1 : 0)

// §35 split recorders — arrays are references, so functions may mutate their CONTENTS (the
// no-global-writes rule blocks only scalar reassignment). One slot per tier / context flag.
f_recSlot(int idx, float r) =>
    array.set(stCtxN, idx, array.get(stCtxN, idx) + 1)
    array.set(stCtxW, idx, array.get(stCtxW, idx) + (r > 0.05 ? 1 : 0))
    array.set(stCtxR, idx, array.get(stCtxR, idx) + r)

f_recCtx(Trade t, float r) =>
    f_recSlot(t.tier == "A+" ? 0 : t.tier == "A" ? 1 : t.tier == "B" ? 2 : 3, r)
    if t.cCrt
        f_recSlot(4, r)
    if t.cTbs
        f_recSlot(5, r)
    if t.cKz
        f_recSlot(6, r)
    if t.cNews
        f_recSlot(7, r)

// blended realized R when a TP1 partial was taken: the TP1 fraction locked its own fill-R,
// the remainder rides to the final exit. Without a partial the final R passes through.
f_blendR(Trade t, float finalR) =>
    not t.tp1Done or na(t.tp1R) ? finalR : t.tp1R * (trTp1Pct / 100.0) + finalR * (1.0 - trTp1Pct / 100.0)

bool tpHit  = false
bool slHit  = false
bool exHit  = false
bool tsHit  = false
bool tp1Now = false

// manage BEFORE opening a new one, so a fresh signal on the exit bar can still open
if trade.dir != 0
    // both levels touched in one bar → assume the STOP filled first. Intrabar order is
    // unknowable; v2 assumed TP and silently inflated the apparent win rate.
    // excursion tracking, in R off the FROZEN initial risk — before any exit test, so the
    // bar that stops the trade out still contributes its own adverse and favourable extremes
    if not na(trade.risk) and trade.risk > 0
        trade.mfe := math.max(trade.mfe, (trade.dir == 1 ? high - trade.entry : trade.entry - low) / trade.risk)
        trade.mae := math.min(trade.mae, (trade.dir == 1 ? low - trade.entry : trade.entry - high) / trade.risk)
    bool tpTouch = trade.dir == 1 ? high >= trade.target : low  <= trade.target
    bool slTouch = trade.dir == 1 ? low  <= trade.stop   : high >= trade.stop
    slHit := slTouch
    tpHit := tpTouch and not slTouch
    // TP1 PARTIAL — gap-aware fill; the SL-first tie rule extends here: no partial is
    // granted on a bar that also touched the stop (intrabar order is unknowable — assume
    // the worst). A bar that runs through TP1 straight to the full target still books the
    // partial: price passed through the level on the way.
    if trPartial and not trade.tp1Done and not slTouch and not na(trade.risk) and trade.risk > 0
        float tp1Px = trade.dir == 1 ? trade.entry + trade.risk * trTp1R : trade.entry - trade.risk * trTp1R
        if trade.dir == 1 ? high >= tp1Px : low <= tp1Px
            float tp1Fill = trade.dir == 1 ? math.max(tp1Px, open) : math.min(tp1Px, open)
            trade.tp1Done := true
            trade.tp1R    := (tp1Fill - trade.entry) * trade.dir / trade.risk
            tp1Now        := true
            if not tpHit and canDraw
                array.push(sigLbs, label.new(bar_index, trade.dir == 1 ? high : low, "TP1", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(cBull, 100), textcolor = color.new(cBull, 10), style = trade.dir == 1 ? label.style_label_down : label.style_label_up, size = lblSzSm))
                f_trimLabels(sigLbs, 24)
    // structure exit per mode: external breaks always count (unless Off); an opposing
    // INTERNAL CHoCH counts per the chosen strictness — raw 3-bar internal wiggles were
    // cutting winners long before the target ("Internal + displacement" default).
    bool extOpp = trade.dir == 1 ? (extE.chDn or extE.bosDn) : (extE.chUp or extE.bosUp)
    bool intOpp = trade.dir == 1 ? intE.chDn : intE.chUp
    // EXIT HIERARCHY (v1.4.0 §21). Order of authority: hard SL · structural invalidation ·
    // opposing HIGH-QUALITY structure · TP1 · final target · trailing/BE. An INTERNAL CHoCH is
    // the weakest structural event in the file — three bars of wiggle — and it was allowed to
    // close a position that had already banked TP1 or moved to break-even, i.e. a trade the
    // market had already proven. Once a position is proven, only an EXTERNAL break (or the
    // target, or the stop) may end it; before it is proven, the internal exit still applies at
    // whatever strictness the user configured. This is a hierarchy, not a new filter: no exit
    // was removed, one was demoted to where it belongs.
    bool proven   = trade.beMoved or trade.tp1Done
    bool intOk    = intOpp and not proven
    bool oppShift = trExitMode == "Off" ? false : trExitMode == "External only" ? extOpp : trExitMode == "Internal any" ? extOpp or intOk : extOpp or (intOk and displacement)
    if not tpHit and not slHit and oppShift and barstate.isconfirmed
        exHit := true
    // TIME STOP — a trade that has gone nowhere for N bars is dead inventory: the zone
    // reacted, the impulse never came, and the open position blocks the cooldown against
    // the next signal. Exit at close when at least trTimeStop bars old and still below the
    // R floor. OFF by default (0).
    if trTimeStop > 0 and not tpHit and not slHit and not exHit and barstate.isconfirmed
        float curTsR = na(trade.risk) or trade.risk <= 0 ? na : (close - trade.entry) * trade.dir / trade.risk
        if not na(curTsR) and bar_index - trade.openBar >= trTimeStop and curTsR < trTimeStopR
            tsHit := true
    // break-even move at +1R — on ACCEPTANCE (v1.1.0): a CLOSE at/beyond +1R, or the TP1
    // partial already banked. A wick touch of +1R followed by a normal pullback used to
    // scratch the trade at entry while the setup was still perfectly valid.
    if trBE and not trade.beMoved and not tpHit and not slHit and not exHit and not tsHit
        float r1 = nz(trade.risk, math.abs(trade.entry - trade.stop))
        bool beAccept = trade.dir == 1 ? close >= trade.entry + r1 : close <= trade.entry - r1
        if (beAccept and barstate.isconfirmed) or trade.tp1Done
            trade.stop    := trade.entry
            trade.beMoved := true
    if tpHit or slHit or exHit or tsHit
        string tag = tpHit ? "TP" : slHit ? (trade.beMoved ? "BE" : "SL") : tsHit ? "⌛" : "✕"
        color  tc  = tpHit ? cBull : slHit and not trade.beMoved ? cBear : cNeut
        if canDraw
            array.push(sigLbs, label.new(bar_index, trade.dir == 1 ? high : low, tag, xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(tc, 100), textcolor = color.new(tc, 10), style = trade.dir == 1 ? label.style_label_down : label.style_label_up, size = lblSz))
            f_trimLabels(sigLbs, 24)
        // realized R off the frozen initial risk. GAP-AWARE: a bar that OPENS beyond the
        // level fills at the open, not the level — v3.5 recorded −1R on a bar that gapped
        // 30 points through the stop while the account took −4R.
        float exitPx = tpHit ? (trade.dir == 1 ? math.max(trade.target, open) : math.min(trade.target, open)) : slHit ? (trade.dir == 1 ? math.min(trade.stop, open) : math.max(trade.stop, open)) : close
        if not na(trade.risk) and trade.risk > 0
            // blended when a TP1 partial was taken — the runner's R alone is not the trade's R
            float rlz = f_blendR(trade, (exitPx - trade.entry) * trade.dir / trade.risk)
            f_recTrade(stats, rlz, trade.dir, trade.mae, trade.mfe)
            f_recCtx(trade, rlz)
        // full stop-out feeds the daily circuit breaker (break-even exits are scratches)
        if slHit and not trade.beMoved
            daySlLoss := daySlLoss + 1
        // full stop-out (not break-even) → the entry zone failed; ban every overlapping zone
        if slHit and not trade.beMoved and enBanLoser and not na(trade.zTop)
            f_banRegion(obUp, trade.zTop, trade.zBot)
            f_banRegion(obDn, trade.zTop, trade.zBot)
            f_banRegion(rbUp, trade.zTop, trade.zBot)
            f_banRegion(rbDn, trade.zTop, trade.zBot)
            f_banRegion(fvUp, trade.zTop, trade.zBot)
            f_banRegion(fvDn, trade.zTop, trade.zBot)
            f_banRegion(htObU, trade.zTop, trade.zBot)
            f_banRegion(htObD, trade.zTop, trade.zBot)
            f_banRegion(htFv,  trade.zTop, trade.zBot)
        f_closeTradeLines(trade)
        trade.dir := 0
    if trade.dir != 0 and trShow and heavyOk
        if not na(trade.lnE)
            line.set_x2(trade.lnE, bar_index)
        if not na(trade.lnS)
            line.set_x2(trade.lnS, bar_index)
            line.set_y1(trade.lnS, trade.stop)
            line.set_y2(trade.lnS, trade.stop)
        if not na(trade.lnT)
            line.set_x2(trade.lnT, bar_index)

if entryBuy or entrySell
    bool dirB = entryBuy
    if dirB
        lastBuyBar := bar_index
    else
        lastSellBar := bar_index
    // EVERY model carries its OWN score now (v1.0.6): sequence = qBuy/qSell · BRT = arm+retest
    // facts · trap = excursion confluence. Everything
    // downstream — grade, tier stats, alert text — reads the one lastScore field.
    lastScore := entryBrt ? (dirB ? brScLNow : brScSNow) : entryTrap ? (dirB ? trapScB : trapScS) : dirB ? qBuy : qSell
    // ENTRY QUALITY GRADE per model (v1.1.0): sequence = structural grade · BRT = 0-5
    // structural grade · trap = trap grade + score
    string entryGrade = entryBrt ? f_gradeBr(dirB ? brQL : brQS) : entryTrap ? f_gradeTrap(dirB ? trapQB : trapQS, int(lastScore)) : dirB ? gradeB : gradeS
    Setup su  = dirB ? suBuy : suSell
    // the liquidity event this print rests on — the duplicate guard reads it next bar
    if dirB
        lastEvtB := entryBrt or entryTrap ? bar_index : nz(su.sweepBar, bar_index)
    else
        lastEvtS := entryBrt or entryTrap ? bar_index : nz(su.sweepBar, bar_index)
    Setup opp = dirB ? suSell : suBuy
    // only a SEQUENCE entry spends the sequence — BRT / trap entries leave it alone
    if not entryBrt and not entryTrap
        su.state := SU_IDLE      // the sequence is spent by the PRINTED signal, not the raw trigger
    // LIFECYCLE HYGIENE (v1.1.0): a printed entry is a confirmed structural event in ITS
    // direction — a live opposing sequence (armed or shifted) is contradicted by it. Reset it
    // instead of letting two conflicting long/short machines run into each other.
    opp.state := SU_IDLE
    // STOP anchor per model: sequence = the FARTHER of sweep extreme and tapped-zone far edge
    // (v1.1.0 — the stop must sit beyond both invalidations) · BRT = retest extreme · trap =
    // excursion extreme. Always the defended level ± buffer.
    float seqAnchor = dirB ? math.min(su.sweepExt, nz(tapBotB, su.sweepExt)) : math.max(su.sweepExt, nz(tapTopS, su.sweepExt))
    float anchor  = entryBrt ? (dirB ? brExtL : brExtS) : entryTrap ? (dirB ? fbBullExt : fbBearExt) : seqAnchor
    float  stopPx = dirB ? anchor - atr * trStopBuf : anchor + atr * trStopBuf
    string srcTag = entryBrt ? "BRT" : entryTrap ? "TRAP·" + (dirB ? fbBullTag : fbBearTag) : su.sweepTag
    // every label prints the model's own score AND its structural grade
    string tagTxt = str.tostring(math.round(lastScore)) + "\n" + entryGrade + " · " + srcTag
    // ── ENTRY FILL (v1.0.3): simulate the limit order a sniper would have had resting at the
    //    POI. Sequence entries fill per the ⑫ fill mode (the trigger already proved the wick
    //    reached the zone — the fill is historical fact); BRT fills at the broken level when
    //    the dig reached it (trigger close when the
    //    dig only came within tolerance); trap entries always fill at the reclaim close (no
    //    pre-known level exists). min(open, level) for buys: a gap through fills at the open.
    float entryPx = close
    if entryBrt
        float brFillLvl = dirB ? brLvlL : brLvlS
        entryPx := dirB ? (low <= brFillLvl ? math.min(open, brFillLvl) : close) : (high >= brFillLvl ? math.max(open, brFillLvl) : close)
        // the printed entry SPENDS the watch (a vetoed trigger keeps it — same rule as the sequence)
        if dirB
            brLvlL := na
        else
            brLvlS := na
    if not entryTrap and not entryBrt and trFillMode != "Trigger close"
        float zT = dirB ? tapTopB : tapTopS
        float zB = dirB ? tapBotB : tapBotS
        if not na(zT) and not na(zB)
            float ceL = (zT + zB) / 2.0
            if dirB
                entryPx := trFillMode == "Zone CE (50%)" and low <= ceL ? math.min(open, ceL) : math.min(open, zT)
            else
                entryPx := trFillMode == "Zone CE (50%)" and high >= ceL ? math.max(open, ceL) : math.max(open, zB)
    float risk   = math.max(math.abs(entryPx - stopPx), atr * 0.25)
    float tgtPx  = dirB ? entryPx + risk * trRR : entryPx - risk * trRR
    // Cap-target mode: the realistic draw ends just inside the nearest wall — clamp there
    // (the gate already verified the clamped RR clears the minimum)
    if enObstMode == "Cap target" and enObstMult > 0
        if dirB and not na(capTgtB)
            tgtPx := math.min(tgtPx, capTgtB)
        if not dirB and not na(capTgtS)
            tgtPx := math.max(tgtPx, capTgtS)
    color sCol   = dirB ? cBull : cBear
    // WARNING mode prints the signal but marks it — the trader sees the volatility risk on the
    // chart itself, not only in the alert payload.
    string riskTag = newsWarn ? "\n⚠ NEWS RISK" : ""
    if canDraw
        // fully solid signal chip (v1.0.7) — 12% transparency let candles bleed through the text
        array.push(sigLbs, label.new(bar_index, dirB ? low : high, (dirB ? "BUY" : "SELL") + "  " + tagTxt + riskTag, xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(sCol, 0), textcolor = color.white, style = dirB ? label.style_label_up : label.style_label_down, size = lblSz))
        f_trimLabels(sigLbs, 24)
    // replace any live trade — record the forced exit (label + exHit alert + stats) so anyone
    // following alerts is not silently left holding the old position
    if trade.dir != 0
        exHit := true
        if not na(trade.risk) and trade.risk > 0
            float rlz = f_blendR(trade, (close - trade.entry) * trade.dir / trade.risk)
            f_recTrade(stats, rlz, trade.dir, trade.mae, trade.mfe)
            f_recCtx(trade, rlz)
        if canDraw
            array.push(sigLbs, label.new(bar_index, trade.dir == 1 ? high : low, "✕", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(cNeut, 100), textcolor = color.new(cNeut, 10), style = trade.dir == 1 ? label.style_label_down : label.style_label_up, size = lblSz))
            f_trimLabels(sigLbs, 24)
    f_closeTradeLines(trade)
    trade.dir     := dirB ? 1 : -1
    trade.entry   := entryPx
    trade.stop    := stopPx
    trade.target  := tgtPx
    trade.risk    := risk
    trade.openBar := bar_index
    trade.beMoved := false
    trade.tp1Done := false
    trade.tp1R    := na
    trade.mae     := 0.0
    trade.mfe     := 0.0
    dayTrades     := dayTrades + 1
    // entry zone bounds → ban rule on stop-out (BRT / trap / CRT entries have no zone — na skips the ban)
    trade.zTop    := entryBrt or entryTrap ? na : (dirB ? tapTopB : tapTopS)
    trade.zBot    := entryBrt or entryTrap ? na : (dirB ? tapBotB : tapBotS)
    // §35 context, frozen at entry — the per-tier / per-filter stat splits read these at close
    trade.tier    := entryGrade
    trade.model   := srcTag
    trade.cCrt    := dirB ? crtLiveBull : crtLiveBear
    trade.cTbs    := dirB ? tbsAlignB : tbsAlignS
    trade.cKz     := sessOk and (anyKz or inSilver)
    trade.cNews   := newsWarn
    // liquidity path frozen at entry (v1.1.0) — internal wall · external level · HTF level
    trade.tpInt   := dirB ? tpIntB : tpIntS
    trade.tpExt   := dirB ? tpExtB : tpExtS
    trade.tpHtf   := dirB ? tpHtfB : tpHtfS
    if trShow and canDraw
        trade.lnE := line.new(bar_index, entryPx, bar_index + 1, entryPx, xloc = xloc.bar_index, color = color.new(cInk, 35),  style = line.style_solid,  width = 1)
        trade.lnS := line.new(bar_index, stopPx, bar_index + 1, stopPx, xloc = xloc.bar_index, color = color.new(cBear, 30), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)
        trade.lnT := line.new(bar_index, tgtPx,  bar_index + 1, tgtPx,  xloc = xloc.bar_index, color = color.new(cBull, 30), style = line.style_dotted, width = 1)

// Setup-stage notifications. NOTE: `suBuy.state[1]` would NOT work — Pine's history operator
// on a mutated object returns the same instance, so field history is never tracked. Copy the
// state into a plain int series and take history on THAT.
int buyStateNow  = suBuy.state
int sellStateNow = suSell.state
buyArmed  = buyStateNow  == SU_SWEPT   and buyStateNow[1]  != SU_SWEPT
sellArmed = sellStateNow == SU_SWEPT   and sellStateNow[1] != SU_SWEPT
buyConf   = buyStateNow  == SU_SHIFTED and buyStateNow[1]  != SU_SHIFTED
sellConf  = sellStateNow == SU_SHIFTED and sellStateNow[1] != SU_SHIFTED

// Stage markers — the sequence made visible. ① = liquidity swept (setup armed), ② = structure
// shifted (retrace watch). CHIP LABELS, not plotshapes: a tiny colored glyph drawn on top of
// a colored shape was an unreadable blob on the dark chart — a solid dark chip with a full-
// opacity glyph reads at any zoom. Commit on bar close only — non-repainting.
var array<label> stgLbs = array.new<label>()
if enStageMk and barstate.isconfirmed and canDraw
    if buyArmed
        array.push(stgLbs, label.new(bar_index, low,  "①", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(cPanel, 0), textcolor = color.new(cBull, 0), style = label.style_label_up,   size = lblSz))
    if sellArmed
        array.push(stgLbs, label.new(bar_index, high, "①", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(cPanel, 0), textcolor = color.new(cBear, 0), style = label.style_label_down, size = lblSz))
    if buyConf
        array.push(stgLbs, label.new(bar_index, low,  "②", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(cPanel, 0), textcolor = color.new(cBull, 0), style = label.style_label_up,   size = lblSz))
    if sellConf
        array.push(stgLbs, label.new(bar_index, high, "②", xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(cPanel, 0), textcolor = color.new(cBear, 0), style = label.style_label_down, size = lblSz))
    f_trimLabels(stgLbs, 16)

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 19. DASHBOARD
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// (next-entry projection moved BEFORE section 18 — the obstacle veto consumes it)

// lifecycle vocabulary (v1.1.0): ARMED = ① sweep graded · CONFIRMED = ② shift graded
f_sqTxt(int q) => q >= 3 ? "inst" : q == 2 ? "strong" : q == 1 ? "normal" : "weak"
f_shTxt(int q) => q >= 3 ? "MSS" : q == 2 ? "normal" : "weak"
f_suTxt(Setup s) =>
    switch s.state
        SU_SWEPT   => "① ARMED · " + s.sweepTag + " · " + f_sqTxt(s.sweepQ) + (s.edge ? " · edge" : "")
        SU_SHIFTED => "② CONFIRMED · shift " + f_shTxt(s.shiftQ) + " · sweep " + f_sqTxt(s.sweepQ) + (s.why == "" ? "" : " · " + s.why)
        => "—"

// Global scope — Pine forbids function declarations inside local (if) blocks
f_dbRow(table t, int r, string k, string v, color vc, string sz) =>
    table.cell(t, 0, r, k, text_color = color.new(cInk, 40), text_size = sz, text_halign = text.align_left,  bgcolor = color.new(cPanel, 18))
    table.cell(t, 1, r, v, text_color = vc, text_size = sz, text_halign = text.align_right, bgcolor = color.new(cPanel, 18))

// ══ HTF CONTEXT SECTION (v1.5.0 §24) ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//   Replaces the five multi-timeframe EMA bias rows. Those answered "is the 4H close above its
//   4H EMA" five times; these answer the question an operator actually asks before an entry —
//   what has the context timeframe DONE, where is its inventory, and does any of it agree with
//   the trade I am about to take. Every value here is derived from CLOSED context candles.
f_htfPoiTxt(bool dirBull) =>
    float t = dirBull ? htPoiTB : htPoiTS
    float b = dirBull ? htPoiBB : htPoiBS
    na(t) ? "—" : (dirBull ? "▲ " : "▼ ") + htfLbl + " " + (dirBull ? htPoiKB : htPoiKS) + " q" + str.tostring(dirBull ? htPoiQB : htPoiQS) + "/T" + str.tostring(math.max(1, dirBull ? htPoiTchB : htPoiTchS)) + " · " + f_px(b) + "–" + f_px(t)

f_dbHtfRows(table t, int r, string sz) =>
    // ① the context timeframe itself, and how it was chosen. The non-repainting contract is
    //    stated on the row because it is the one property a reader cannot verify by looking.
    f_dbRow(t, r, "HTF TF", not htfTfOk ? "⚠ " + htfLbl + " ≤ chart — OFF" : htfLbl + (htAuto ? " · auto" : " · manual") + " · closed", not htfTfOk ? cBear : color.new(DEF_ACCENT, 12), sz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 1, "HTF Structure", htStrTxt, hx.strQ == 0 or not htStrLive ? cNeut : hx.strD == 1 ? cBull : cBear, sz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 2, "HTF CISD", htCisdTxt, htCisdDir == 1 ? cBull : htCisdDir == -1 ? cBear : cNeut, sz)
    // ② live institutional inventory on the context timeframe, by family and lifecycle state
    f_dbRow(t, r + 3, "HTF Zones", not htfTfOk ? "—" : "FVG " + str.tostring(hx.nFvg) + " · IFVG " + str.tostring(hx.nIfvg) + " · OB " + str.tostring(hx.nOb) + " · BRK " + str.tostring(hx.nBrk) + " · MIT " + str.tostring(hx.nMit), not htfTfOk ? cNeut : color.new(cInk, 20), sz)
    // ③ the actionable POI on each side, ranked structurally (f_htfPoi), plus how far price
    //    still has to travel to reach it — the two numbers that decide whether HTF context is
    //    something to trade INTO or something merely in the way
    f_dbRow(t, r + 4, "HTF POI", htfBias == -1 ? f_htfPoiTxt(false) : f_htfPoiTxt(true), htfBias == -1 ? cBear : cBull, sz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 5, "HTF Distance", na(htPoiDB) and na(htPoiDS) ? "—" : "▲ " + (na(htPoiDB) ? "—" : str.tostring(htPoiDB, "#.##") + " ATR") + "  ·  ▼ " + (na(htPoiDS) ? "—" : str.tostring(htPoiDS, "#.##") + " ATR"), color.new(cInk, 20), sz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 6, "HTF Liquidity", not htfTfOk ? "—" : "BSL " + f_px(htfUp) + " · SSL " + f_px(htfDn), not htfTfOk ? cNeut : color.new(cInk, 20), sz)
    // ④ HOW MUCH of the context agrees, per side. Read it as a gate, not a probability: A+ needs
    //    at least 2 of {bias · structure · CISD · an actionable POI on the correct side}.
    f_dbRow(t, r + 7, "HTF Context", not htfTfOk ? "—" : "▲ " + str.tostring(htCtxB) + "/3  ·  ▼ " + str.tostring(htCtxS) + "/3", not htfTfOk ? cNeut : htCtxB > htCtxS ? cBull : htCtxS > htCtxB ? cBear : cNeut, sz)

// one §35 split slot → "A+ 5tr 60% +0.4R" (or "A+ —" while empty)
f_slotTxt(string nm, int i) =>
    int n = array.get(stCtxN, i)
    n == 0 ? nm + " —" : nm + " " + str.tostring(n) + "tr " + str.tostring(math.round(100.0 * array.get(stCtxW, i) / n)) + "% " + (array.get(stCtxR, i) / n >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(array.get(stCtxR, i) / n, "#.#") + "R"

var table db = na
// Rebuild once per bar close — rebuilding 29 rows of strings on EVERY realtime tick was a
// steady CPU leak. Exception: a LIVE trade keeps per-tick updates so Live P/L stays real-time.
bool dbTick = barstate.isconfirmed or trade.dir != 0 or na(db)
// ══ DASHBOARD RENDERER ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//   The 30-odd rows live in a FUNCTION, not in global scope. Two reasons, both structural:
//   (1) §24 — the trading engine must not share a scope with the visualisation engine; every
//       statement here is presentation, reads engine state and writes none of it, so nothing
//       in the signal path can be affected by a change made in here;
//   (2) Pine's CE10295 'main body too long' counts GLOBAL-SCOPE statements and statements
//       inside a function body are free — this file sits close enough to that cap that the
//       dashboard alone was consuming a meaningful share of the remaining engine headroom.
//   The table OBJECT is still created in global scope: a function may mutate an object but
//   may never assign a global (see the Pine v6 discipline note in the header).
f_drawDash(table t) =>
    string tSz = dbSize == "Compact" ? size.small : size.normal
    table.clear(t, 0, 0, 1, 43)
    table.cell(t, 0, 0, "CRT│TBS BRT v1.5.1", text_color = color.new(cInk, 8), text_size = tSz, text_halign = text.align_left, bgcolor = color.new(cGrid, 25))
    table.cell(t, 1, 0, timeframe.period + " ⟶ " + htfLbl + " · CRT " + f_tfName(crtTf), text_color = color.new(DEF_ACCENT, 12), text_size = tSz, text_halign = text.align_right, bgcolor = color.new(cGrid, 25))
    f_dbRow(t, 1, "HTF Bias", htfBiasTxt, htfBias == 1 ? cBull : htfBias == -1 ? cBear : cNeut, tSz)
    int r = 2
    if dbHtf
        f_dbHtfRows(t, r, tSz)
        r += 8
    f_dbRow(t, r,     "LTF Structure", structTxt, msExt.dir == 1 ? cBull : msExt.dir == -1 ? cBear : cNeut, tSz)
    // MARKET REGIME (v1.1.0): DEAD · RANGE · TRANSITION · TREND · EXPANSION + ADX
    f_dbRow(t, r + 1, "Regime", not rfEnable ? "—" : regimeTxt + " · ADX " + str.tostring(math.round(rfAdx)), not rfEnable ? cNeut : regime == RG_DEAD ? cNeut : regime == RG_RANGE ? DEF_ACCENT : regime == RG_EXP ? DEF_VIOLET : cBull, tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 2, "Prem / Disc", rOk ? (inPremium ? "Premium" : "Discount") + (oteTapNow ? " · OTE" : "") : "—", not rOk ? cNeut : inPremium ? cBear : cBull, tSz)
    // SESSION (v1.5.0 §16): active kill zone AND the last session-extreme raid. "Inside a
    // kill zone" is never treated as directional on its own — it is shown as context, and
    // the sweep that IS the event is named next to it.
    string ssRow = kzTxt + (sessSwpTxt == "—" or na(sessSwpBar) ? "" : " · " + sessSwpTxt + " " + str.tostring(bar_index - sessSwpBar) + "b")
    f_dbRow(t, r + 3, "Session", ssRow, anyKz ? cKzBg : color.new(cInk, 40), tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 4, "Liquidity", liqTxt, color.new(cInk, 30), tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 5, "Buy Setup",  f_suTxt(suBuy),  suBuy.state  != SU_IDLE ? cBull : cNeut, tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 6, "Sell Setup", f_suTxt(suSell), suSell.state != SU_IDLE ? cBear : cNeut, tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 7, "Trade", trade.dir == 0 ? "flat" : (trade.dir == 1 ? "BUY (LONG)" : "SELL (SHORT)") + (trade.beMoved ? " · BE" : "") + (trade.tp1Done ? " · TP1" : ""), trade.dir == 1 ? cBull : trade.dir == -1 ? cBear : cNeut, tSz)
    // live trade numbers — entry / stop / target straight off the tracked trade record
    f_dbRow(t, r + 8,  "Entry @",  trade.dir == 0 ? "—" : f_px(trade.entry),  trade.dir == 0 ? cNeut : color.new(cInk, 10), tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 9,  "Stop @",   trade.dir == 0 ? "—" : f_px(trade.stop),   trade.dir == 0 ? cNeut : cBear, tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 10, "Target @", trade.dir == 0 ? "—" : f_px(trade.target), trade.dir == 0 ? cNeut : cBull, tSz)
    // live P/L in R (initial risk frozen at entry — BE stop move must not distort it) + duration
    // blended when a TP1 partial is booked — the banked fraction is realized, only the runner floats
    float liveR = trade.dir == 0 or na(trade.risk) or trade.risk <= 0 ? na : f_blendR(trade, (trade.dir == 1 ? close - trade.entry : trade.entry - close) / trade.risk)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 11, "Live P/L", na(liveR) ? "—" : (liveR >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(liveR, "#.##") + "R · " + str.tostring(bar_index - trade.openBar) + " bars", na(liveR) ? cNeut : liveR >= 0 ? cBull : cBear, tSz)
    // next candidate entries — nearest fresh zone on the retrace side of current price
    f_dbRow(t, r + 12, "Next BUY zone",  na(nextBuyPx)  ? "—" : f_px(nextBuyPx)  + "  (" + f_px(nxBuyB7)  + "–" + f_px(nxBuyT7)  + ")", na(nextBuyPx)  ? cNeut : cBull, tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 13, "Next SELL zone", na(nextSellPx) ? "—" : f_px(nextSellPx) + "  (" + f_px(nxSellB7) + "–" + f_px(nxSellT7) + ")", na(nextSellPx) ? cNeut : cBear, tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 14, "CRT", crtTxt + (crtHtfB or crtHtfS ? " · in " + htfLbl + " zone" : ""), not crtEnable ? cNeut : crtStale ? DEF_ACCENT : crtLiveBull ? cBull : crtLiveBear ? cBear : cNeut, tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 15, "CRT range", crtEnable ? crtRngTxt : "—", crtEnable ? color.new(DEF_VIOLET, 15) : cNeut, tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 16, "TBS", tbsTxt + (tbsHtfB or tbsHtfS ? " · in " + htfLbl + " zone" : ""), not tbsEnable ? cNeut : tbsDir == 1 ? cBull : tbsDir == -1 ? cBear : tbsInvalid ? DEF_ACCENT : cNeut, tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 17, "Mode", sigMode + (isAgr ? "  ⚠ research" : ""), isAgr ? cBear : isPrec ? DEF_VIOLET : isBal ? cBull : cNeut, tSz)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 18, "News risk", newsTxt, newsBlock ? cBear : newsWarn ? DEF_ACCENT : color.new(cInk, 40), tSz)
    // last blocked trigger — which gate killed it and how long ago
    f_dbRow(t, r + 19, "Reject", lastVetoTxt == "—" ? "—" : lastVetoTxt + " · " + str.tostring(bar_index - lastVetoBar) + " bars ago", lastVetoTxt == "—" ? cNeut : DEF_ACCENT, tSz)
    // failed-breakout state — live while it still earns the weight, then greys out
    string fbRow = not fbEnable ? "off" : fbTxt == "—" ? "—" : fbTxt + " · " + str.tostring(bar_index - lastFbBar) + " bars" + (fbAlignB or fbAlignS ? "" : " · expired")
    f_dbRow(t, r + 20, "Failed breakout", fbRow, not fbEnable ? cNeut : fbAlignB ? cBull : fbAlignS ? cBear : cNeut, tSz)
    // daily circuit breaker — entries and full stop-outs today vs their caps
    string dgRow = rkMaxTrades == 0 and rkMaxLoss == 0 ? "off" : str.tostring(dayTrades) + (rkMaxTrades > 0 ? "/" + str.tostring(rkMaxTrades) : "") + " tr · " + str.tostring(daySlLoss) + (rkMaxLoss > 0 ? "/" + str.tostring(rkMaxLoss) : "") + " SL" + (dayOk ? "" : " · HALTED")
    f_dbRow(t, r + 21, "Day guard", dgRow, rkMaxTrades == 0 and rkMaxLoss == 0 ? cNeut : dayOk ? color.new(cInk, 40) : cBear, tSz)
    // ⑳ armed breakout-retest watch — the broken level awaiting its continuation retest
    string brRow = not brEnable ? "off" : not na(brLvlL) ? "▲ retest " + f_px(brLvlL) + " · " + str.tostring(bar_index - brBarL) + " bars" : not na(brLvlS) ? "▼ retest " + f_px(brLvlS) + " · " + str.tostring(bar_index - brBarS) + " bars" : "—"
    f_dbRow(t, r + 22, "B/R setup", brRow, not brEnable ? cNeut : not na(brLvlL) ? cBull : not na(brLvlS) ? cBear : cNeut, tSz)
    // LIQUIDITY PATH (v1.1.0): live trade → the frozen ladder in R; flat → the ladder from
    // the current close for the side the nearer setup is building
    string lpRow = "—"
    if trade.dir != 0 and not na(trade.risk) and trade.risk > 0
        float rI = na(trade.tpInt) ? na : (trade.tpInt - trade.entry) * trade.dir / trade.risk
        float rE = na(trade.tpExt) ? na : (trade.tpExt - trade.entry) * trade.dir / trade.risk
        float rH = na(trade.tpHtf) ? na : (trade.tpHtf - trade.entry) * trade.dir / trade.risk
        lpRow := "int " + (na(rI) ? "—" : str.tostring(rI, "#.#") + "R") + " · ext " + (na(rE) ? "—" : str.tostring(rE, "#.#") + "R") + " · HTF " + (na(rH) ? "—" : str.tostring(rH, "#.#") + "R")
    if trade.dir == 0
        bool lpBull = suBuy.state >= suSell.state
        lpRow := (lpBull ? "▲ " : "▼ ") + "int " + f_px(lpBull ? tpIntB : tpIntS) + " · ext " + f_px(lpBull ? tpExtB : tpExtS) + " · HTF " + f_px(lpBull ? tpHtfB : tpHtfS)
    f_dbRow(t, r + 23, "Liq path", lpRow, trade.dir == 1 ? cBull : trade.dir == -1 ? cBear : color.new(cInk, 40), tSz)
    // CONFLUENCE MATRIX (v1.2.0) — the structural grades behind the number, for the side with
    // the stronger live setup. It is a CONFLUENCE / SETUP-QUALITY score, never a probability:
    // it RANKS setups the structural gates have already validated.
    bool   qBull = f_gradeRank(gradeB) >= f_gradeRank(gradeS)
    Setup  qSu   = qBull ? suBuy : suSell
    string qRow  = (qBull ? "▲ " : "▼ ") + (qBull ? gradeB : gradeS) + " · Q" + str.tostring(qBull ? qBuy : qSell) + " · htf " + str.tostring(qBull ? htCtxB : htCtxS) + "/3 · swp " + f_sqTxt(qSu.sweepQ) + " · shf " + f_shTxt(qSu.shiftQ) + " · poi " + ((qBull ? tapB.htf : tapS.htf) ? htfLbl + " " : "") + str.tostring(qSu.poiQ) + "/T" + str.tostring(qSu.poiTch) + " · ote " + str.tostring(qSu.oteQ) + " · path " + str.tostring(qBull ? lpqB : lpqS) + "/2 · exec " + str.tostring(qBull ? execQB : execQS) + "/3"
    f_dbRow(t, r + 24, "Confluence", qSu.state == SU_IDLE ? "—" : qRow, qSu.state == SU_IDLE ? cNeut : qBull ? cBull : cBear, tSz)
    // LIQUIDITY EVENT IDENTITY (§3) — ONE causal event, named by however many engines saw it.
    // Reading "SMC+FB+TBS" here means three DESCRIPTORS of one action, never three confluences.
    f_dbRow(t, r + 25, "Liq event", qSu.state == SU_IDLE ? "—" : "#" + str.tostring(nz(qSu.sweepBar, 0)) + " · " + (qSu.evtDesc % 2 >= 1 ? "SMC " : "") + (qSu.evtDesc % 4 >= 2 ? "FB " : "") + (qSu.evtDesc % 8 >= 4 ? "TBS " : "") + (qSu.evtDesc >= 8 ? "CRT" : "") + " · " + str.tostring(bar_index - nz(qSu.sweepBar, bar_index)) + "b", qSu.state == SU_IDLE ? cNeut : color.new(cInk, 20), tSz)
    // closed-trade performance — all-time win rate + average R off the
    // tracked entry/stop/target records (SL-first tie rule + gap-aware fills → conservative)
    if dbStats
        float wAll = stats.n > 0 ? 100.0 * stats.w / stats.n : na
        float rAll = stats.n > 0 ? stats.sumR / stats.n : na
        string allTxt = stats.n == 0 ? "—" : str.tostring(stats.n) + " tr · " + str.tostring(math.round(wAll)) + "% · " + (rAll >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(rAll, "#.##") + "R"
        string bTxt = stats.nB == 0 ? "B —" : "B " + str.tostring(stats.nB) + "tr " + str.tostring(math.round(100.0 * stats.wB / stats.nB)) + "% " + (stats.sumRB / stats.nB >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(stats.sumRB / stats.nB, "#.#") + "R"
        string sTxt = stats.nS == 0 ? "S —" : "S " + str.tostring(stats.nS) + "tr " + str.tostring(math.round(100.0 * stats.wS / stats.nS)) + "% " + (stats.sumRS / stats.nS >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(stats.sumRS / stats.nS, "#.#") + "R"
        f_dbRow(t, r + 26, "Stats (all)",  allTxt, stats.n == 0 ? cNeut : rAll >= 0 ? cBull : cBear, tSz)
        f_dbRow(t, r + 27, "Buy / Sell", stats.n == 0 ? "—" : bTxt + "  ·  " + sTxt, stats.n == 0 ? cNeut : color.new(cInk, 20), tSz)
        // §35 splits — read across a slot: trades · win% · avg R. "By grade" answers whether the
        // structural grade predicts outcomes (A+ should beat B — if it doesn't, the grade
        // model is wrong for this symbol). "By filter" answers whether each context filter
        // genuinely adds value.
        f_dbRow(t, r + 28, "By grade",  stats.n == 0 ? "—" : f_slotTxt("A+", 0) + " · " + f_slotTxt("A", 1) + " · " + f_slotTxt("B", 2), stats.n == 0 ? cNeut : color.new(cInk, 20), tSz)
        f_dbRow(t, r + 29, "By filter", stats.n == 0 ? "—" : f_slotTxt("CRT", 4) + " · " + f_slotTxt("TBS", 5) + " · " + f_slotTxt("KZ", 6) + " · " + f_slotTxt("NWS", 7), stats.n == 0 ? cNeut : color.new(cInk, 20), tSz)
        // MAE / MFE (§22) — the diagnostic that separates a bad SETUP from a bad ENTRY, a stop
        // that is too tight and a target that is too far. Live values shown while a trade runs.
        string exTxt = stats.n == 0 and trade.dir == 0 ? "—" : trade.dir != 0 ? "live " + str.tostring(trade.mae, "#.##") + "R / +" + str.tostring(trade.mfe, "#.##") + "R" : "avg " + str.tostring(stats.sumMae / stats.n, "#.##") + "R / +" + str.tostring(stats.sumMfe / stats.n, "#.##") + "R"
        f_dbRow(t, r + 30, "MAE / MFE", exTxt, stats.n == 0 and trade.dir == 0 ? cNeut : color.new(cInk, 20), tSz)
    r

if dbEnable and barstate.islast and dbTick
    if na(db)
        db := table.new(f_tablePos(dbPos), 2, 44, bgcolor = color.new(cPanel, 8), border_color = color.new(cGrid, 30), border_width = 1, frame_color = color.new(cGrid, 20), frame_width = 1)
    f_drawDash(db)

// ── On-chart live position tag — the CURRENTLY RUNNING entry, always visible at the right
//    edge while a trade is open (independent of the dashboard toggle)
var label posLb = na
if barstate.islast
    if trade.dir != 0
        string ptxt = (trade.dir == 1 ? "▲ LONG (BUY)" : "▼ SHORT (SELL)") + (trade.beMoved ? " · BE" : "") + (trade.tp1Done ? " · TP1" : "") + "\nEntry   " + f_px(trade.entry) + "\nStop    " + f_px(trade.stop) + "\nTarget  " + f_px(trade.target)
        color pCol = trade.dir == 1 ? cBull : cBear
        if na(posLb)
            posLb := label.new(bar_index + 8, trade.entry, ptxt, xloc = xloc.bar_index, yloc = yloc.price, color = color.new(pCol, 15), textcolor = color.white, style = label.style_label_left, size = lblSzSm)
        label.set_xy(posLb, bar_index + 8, trade.entry)
        label.set_text(posLb, ptxt)
        label.set_color(posLb, color.new(pCol, 15))
    if trade.dir == 0 and not na(posLb)
        label.delete(posLb)
        posLb := na

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 20. ALERTS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Event alertconditions AND barstate.isconfirmed: a developing bar can satisfy (and later
// un-satisfy) these mid-bar, so 'Once per bar' alert settings fired on values that never
// committed — worst case tpHit, whose verdict can FLIP intrabar (TP touch first, SL touch
// later → the SL-first tie rule wins at close). The entry conditions and every alert() call
// were already close-gated; this makes the whole alert surface uniform.
alertcondition(barstate.isconfirmed and (extE.bosUp or extE.bosDn), "BOS",            "BOS · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(barstate.isconfirmed and (extE.chUp or extE.chDn),   "CHoCH / MSS",    "CHoCH/MSS · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(barstate.isconfirmed and (cisdBull or cisdBear),     "CISD",           "CISD · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(barstate.isconfirmed and buyArmed,                 "Buy Setup Armed",   "① Sellside swept — buy setup armed · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(barstate.isconfirmed and sellArmed,                "Sell Setup Armed",  "① Buyside swept — sell setup armed · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(barstate.isconfirmed and buyConf,                  "Buy Setup Shifted", "② Bullish shift after sweep · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(barstate.isconfirmed and sellConf,                 "Sell Setup Shifted","② Bearish shift after sweep · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(entryBuy,                 "Entry BUY",      "BUY · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(entrySell,                "Entry SELL",     "SELL · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(crtBullOk and not crtBullOk[1], "CRT Bullish", "CRT bullish (range low swept + reclaimed) · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(crtBearOk and not crtBearOk[1], "CRT Bearish", "CRT bearish (range high swept + reclaimed) · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(fbBullEvt or fbBearEvt,   "Failed Breakout", "Failed breakout — level defended · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(barstate.isconfirmed and ((not na(brLvlL) and brBarL == bar_index) or (not na(brLvlS) and brBarS == bar_index)), "BRT Armed", "⑳ Breakout-retest watch armed · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(tbsBullEvt,               "TBS Long",       "TBS bullish setup confirmed · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(tbsBearEvt,               "TBS Short",      "TBS bearish setup confirmed · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(newsWindow and not newsWindow[1], "News Risk Window", "News risk window open · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(anyKz and not anyKz[1],   "Session Start",  "Kill zone opened · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(barstate.isconfirmed and tpHit, "Target Hit", "TP · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(barstate.isconfirmed and slHit, "Stop Hit",   "SL · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(barstate.isconfirmed and tp1Now, "TP1 Partial",  "TP1 partial filled · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(barstate.isconfirmed and tsHit,  "Time Stop Exit", "Time stop exit · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(exHit,                    "Structure Exit", "Exit · {{ticker}} {{interval}}")

if alEnable
    if alStruct and (extE.bosUp or extE.bosDn or extE.chUp or extE.chDn)
        alert(structTxt + " · " + syminfo.ticker + " " + timeframe.period, alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
    if alSetup and (buyArmed or sellArmed)
        alert("① " + (buyArmed ? "Buy" : "Sell") + " setup armed (" + (buyArmed ? suBuy.sweepTag : suSell.sweepTag) + " swept) · " + syminfo.ticker + " " + timeframe.period, alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
    if alSetup and (buyConf or sellConf)
        alert("② " + (buyConf ? "Buy" : "Sell") + " setup shifted · " + syminfo.ticker + " " + timeframe.period, alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
    if alSetup and (tbsBullEvt or tbsBearEvt)
        alert("TBS " + (tbsBullEvt ? "bullish" : "bearish") + " confirmed · Q" + str.tostring(tbsScore) + " · " + syminfo.ticker + " " + timeframe.period, alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
    if alSetup and barstate.isconfirmed and ((not na(brLvlL) and brBarL == bar_index) or (not na(brLvlS) and brBarS == bar_index))
        alert("⑳ BRT armed — retest watch at " + f_px(not na(brLvlL) and brBarL == bar_index ? brLvlL : brLvlS) + " · " + syminfo.ticker + " " + timeframe.period, alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
    if alEntry and (entryBuy or entrySell)
        string alModel = trade.model + " " + str.tostring(math.round(lastScore)) + " " + trade.tier
        // full alert context per the spec: symbol · timeframe · setup · direction · score ·
        // grade · HTF bias · regime · session · news risk · CRT / TBS state · liquidity path
        string alCtx = " · HTF " + htfLbl + " " + htfBiasTxt + " ctx " + str.tostring(entryBuy ? htCtxB : htCtxS) + "/3" + (hx.strD == 0 or not htStrLive ? "" : " · " + hx.strT) + (htCisdDir == 0 ? "" : " · CISD " + (htCisdDir == 1 ? "▲" : "▼")) + " · " + regimeTxt + " · " + (anyKz ? kzTxt : "no KZ") + " · news " + (newsBlock ? "BLOCK" : newsWarn ? "RISK" : "clear") + " · CRT " + (crtLiveBull ? "bull" : crtLiveBear ? "bear" : crtStale ? "stale" : "—") + " · TBS " + (tbsAlignB ? "bull" : tbsAlignS ? "bear" : "—") + " · FB " + (fbAlignB ? "bull" : fbAlignS ? "bear" : "—")
        string alPath = " · path int " + f_px(trade.tpInt) + " / ext " + f_px(trade.tpExt) + " / HTF " + f_px(trade.tpHtf)
        alert((entryBuy ? "BUY " : "SELL ") + alModel + (newsWarn ? " ⚠ NEWS RISK" : "") + " · entry " + str.tostring(trade.entry, format.mintick) + " · stop " + str.tostring(trade.stop, format.mintick) + " · tgt " + str.tostring(trade.target, format.mintick) + alPath + alCtx + " · " + syminfo.ticker + " " + timeframe.period, alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
    if alManage and (tpHit or slHit or exHit or tsHit or tp1Now)
        alert((tpHit ? "TP hit" : slHit ? "SL hit" : tsHit ? "Time stop" : exHit ? "Structure exit" : "TP1 partial filled") + " · " + syminfo.ticker + " " + timeframe.period, alert.freq_once_per_bar_close)
